<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>CIVILIZATIONAL SCALE FRAMEWORK (CSF)</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;
	gap: 46px;
}

.column {
	min-width: 0;
	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {
	display: block;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	line-height: 1.3;
	padding: 0.125rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {
	margin-left: 1.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-2 {
	margin-left: 3rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-3 {
	margin-left: 4.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-link {
	text-decoration: none;
	opacity: 0.7;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.18);
}

table,
th,
td {
	border: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
	border-collapse: collapse;
}

table {
	border-left: none;
	border-right: none;
}

th,
td {
	font-weight: normal;
	padding: 0.25em 0.5em;
	line-height: 1.5;
	min-height: 1.5em;
	text-align: left;
}

th {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
}

ol,
ul {
	margin: 0;
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
	margin-block-end: 0.6em;
}

li > ol:first-child,
li > ul:first-child {
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
}

ul > li {
	list-style: disc;
}

ul.to-do-list {
	padding-inline-start: 0;
}

ul.to-do-list > li {
	list-style: none;
}

.to-do-children-checked {
	text-decoration: line-through;
	opacity: 0.375;
}

ul.toggle > li {
	list-style: none;
}

ul {
	padding-inline-start: 1.7em;
}

ul > li {
	padding-left: 0.1em;
}

ol {
	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-2 {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-3plus {
	padding-inline-start: 2.4em;
}

ol > li {
	padding-left: 0.2em;
}

.mono ol {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

.mono ol > li {
	text-indent: -0.4em;
}

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;
	list-style-type: none;
}

/* Indent toggle children */
.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;
}

.selected-value {
	display: inline-block;
	padding: 0 0.5em;
	background: rgba(206, 205, 202, 0.5);
	border-radius: 3px;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
	margin-top: 0.3em;
	margin-bottom: 0.3em;

}

.collection-title {

	margin-right: 1em;
}

.page-description {
	margin-bottom: 2em;
}

.simple-table {

}
.simple-table td {
	height: 29px;

}

	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table-header-color {

}

	font-weight: 500;
}

time {

}

.icon {

}

}

.callout h2,

.user-icon {

	border-radius: 100%;
	margin-right: 0.5rem;
}

.user-icon-inner {
	font-size: 0.8em;
}

.text-icon {
	border: 1px solid #000;
	text-align: center;
}

.page-cover-image {
	display: block;
	object-fit: cover;
	width: 100%;
	max-height: 30vh;
}

.page-header-icon {
	font-size: 3rem;

}

.page-header-icon-with-cover {
	margin-top: -0.72em;

}

.page-header-icon img {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.link-to-page {
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border: none;
	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border-radius: 0;
	text-align: center;
}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;
	border-radius: 3px;
	font-size: 85%;
	tab-size: 2;
}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;
	color: inherit;
}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	width: 16;
	height: 16;
	background-size: 16px;
	margin-left: 2px;
	margin-right: 5px;
}

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}

.checkbox-off {

</style></head><body><article id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-92a6-eb93f6c17c58" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">CIVILIZATIONAL SCALE FRAMEWORK (CSF)</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bb46-e0d7b3c70a04"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-8f66-c35911cf940b" class="">A Formal Multi-Layer Stability and Compounding Model</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-83c8-cfccf799fe05"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a348-fe89a71dad2d" class="">I. Definition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-852a-c772251a76fa" class="">A civilization is defined as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-b949-c09b3c773605" class="">A persistent, multi-agent coordination system capable of producing surplus, maintaining order, compounding value, and surviving shocks across time.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-8ad3-f7314403236a" class="">We model civilization as a dynamic system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a7e7-df3796aedb6c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C(t) = \{E(t), S(t), L(t), V(t), T(t), I(t), R(t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-bc3e-f00dc06deb22" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-ba3e-e9966d82aec2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Energy &amp; production base</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a314-e7cfd8587214" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Surplus generation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-9482-efc6c579829b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Legitimacy &amp; governance stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b1bf-c3b4cd4dd8d8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Value storage integrity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-a457-f83a9c05e4ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Trust &amp; social coherence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-9293-ccd3525e55dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Institutional capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-812b-d1330898297b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Resilience under stress</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-846e-ff098cb37cd9" class="">Civilizational scale = ability of this system to maintain positive compounding over long horizons.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-afdf-d31fe9e84655"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9a77-ed6d511d689f" class="">II. Core Civilizational Invariant</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-b5cb-dfa3c8ab4ab3" class="">A civilization compounds if and only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-88c5-f0a61460acd3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dW}{dt} &gt; 0 \quad \text{and} \quad P_{collapse} &lt; \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-b005-f8ab7a552d14" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-85b3-edada6c68a98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= Durable wealth (not nominal)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8445-c5fa978b5aec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= systemic failure probability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-80f0-e50728fc573e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= tolerable instability threshold</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-9879-c829292bcfcd" class="">Durable wealth is defined as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-963f-e457aa4e130b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W = S \cdot V \cdot T \cdot L</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-831f-f454ee2bd2fd" class="">If any component → 0, compounding collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-a705-db58fab73c36"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-b42a-db85d07a24b0" class="">III. The 10-Layer Civilizational Stack (Formalized)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-b3ad-e6418b323b7b" class="">Each layer is a constraint boundary.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-b082-e229e5e4d695"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-9991-d92d849300a3" class="">Layer 1: Energy Base</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-a5e7-d4c430d2fec5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E = f(\text{Energy Supply}, \text{Energy Cost}, \text{Energy Reliability})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a0c8-e6da42c73647" class="">Energy Constraint Premium:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-b360-ea8ed882dcca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ECP = \frac{P(\text{Supply Shock}) \cdot D_{inelastic}}{SpareCapacity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-900c-d7461badf76e" class="">If  ↑ → system fragility ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b76d-cc39ddf66c34"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8841-fa3b75d2f99d" class="">Layer 2: Production Capacity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-83d1-ef5461a7fed1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t = A_t \cdot K_t^\alpha \cdot H_t^\beta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-b484-d1ccecbad787" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8b9d-da7aa5dedc4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= technology</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-a190-faac54f8a365" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= capital</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b109-c12bbb1aa3e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= human capability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-8af7-e52b75696595"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-a169-e3b696f0b44d" class="">Layer 3: Surplus Formation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a9ee-d0240f42a20e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t = P_t - C_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-8e2e-e10d0d567706" class="">If  → no civilizational expansion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-80b1-d67505d74211"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b16d-e198b4b8e1e7" class="">Layer 4: Surplus Storage Integrity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-948b-fc51912065e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K_{t+1} = K_t + \psi S_t - \delta K_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-9539-db1bc63cba84" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-b92a-d861062c3385" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= reinvestment efficiency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-98e2-c23a9e5271eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= decay / corruption / inflation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8d9b-dc9c13685c76"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-82b3-c8c75687a4a9" class="">Layer 5: Claim Credibility</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-8222-fade77b03905" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t = \sigma(\alpha_0 + \alpha_1 E_t + \alpha_2 V_t - \alpha_3 N_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-b02e-d7574e021077" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-96a0-ea2f54132640" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= trust in claims (currency, contracts)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-bf00-c348d447e413" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= noise / corruption</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-af15-e1681e653b10" class="">If , capital flight begins.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-ade6-dc368c9836f1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-9b8e-f803c7476676" class="">Layer 6: Credit Expansion</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-9430-e86c92a9b804" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_t = L_{min} + (L_{max} - L_{min}) Q_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-8ed2-dfec3af812a9" class="">Credit scales with credibility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80de-ad81-d67151a73cea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-b6af-e4f8c832df8c" class="">Layer 7: Leverage–Noise Bubble Dynamics</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-970c-d7cc0f1361c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1} = B_t + \gamma_1 L_t (1 - Q_t) + \gamma_2 N_t - \gamma_3 V_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-b399-cdb5308bf2d4" class="">Bubbles form when leverage + low credibility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b896-cd0e46eed545"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-bee0-f51632ed5eca" class="">Layer 8: Institutional Legitimacy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-93de-d2e42e842447" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_{legit} = f(\text{Enforcement}, \text{Fairness}, \text{Predictability})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-a8eb-d4b504c2be6b" class="">Collapse probability increases when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-82f2-c1a8cfb4f0bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dL_{legit}}{dt} &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b8e9-dd48fb51ad1f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-b71d-dcbef9ba5e02" class="">Layer 9: Trust Coherence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-b479-f11c03085a0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T = 1 - FragmentationIndex</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-811d-c78688855a7d" class="">Fragmentation modeled as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-9dd2-f9bea58399fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F = (1 - SharedNarrative) \cdot Polarization \cdot Inequality</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a3dc-c4263ba79e79"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-ac63-e1b8f6ce3cf8" class="">Layer 10: Shock Absorption Capacity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-aff3-ce169dd9897c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R = \frac{BufferCapacity}{ShockMagnitude}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-9a1a-ed806364a1cc" class="">If , systemic cascade begins.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-8d2e-d658f8df0e57"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-a4fa-fbfe976f128d" class="">IV. Civilizational Compounding Equation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9621-ed73c6018f9a" class="">Full state evolution:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-874f-d5cf1c499af6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dC}{dt} = f(E, S, K, Q, L_{legit}, T, R) - \Phi(N, B, F)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8fbc-dbc9b2324961" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-bcbb-ca1c27f0d2f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= entropy forces:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a93f-d61ae5958e41" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">corruption</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-89ac-d8abaef171c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">inequality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b194-faae472faf14" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">fragmentation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-8907-c9a905953176" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">leverage excess</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-84a5-d14b7fbabb63" class="">Civilization survives when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b628-cff617706e24" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\int_{0}^{T} f(...) dt &gt; \int_{0}^{T} \Phi(...) dt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a074-c01d96f51d29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-94d8-e7358d3f59ca" class="">V. Civilizational Scale Index (CSI)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-aa98-d5a130c8573e" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-b529-cd01beb2709c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CSI = \omega_1 E + \omega_2 S + \omega_3 Q + \omega_4 L_{legit} + \omega_5 T + \omega_6 R - \omega_7 B - \omega_8 N</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-9b62-e43f60a23e3b" class="">Where weights  reflect importance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-bc33-e2666590b522" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-8d20-d7d24d844dab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CSI &gt; \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-90f8-d41d80164236" class="">Civilization is compounding.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-ba10-e0aafcd5dc5c" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-a746-d1fac5b942d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CSI &lt; \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-85e4-c9944060c7e5" class="">Decay regime.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-b8c2-d5c1ea75d977"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-958b-cca9aa279864" class="">VI. Collapse Trigger Condition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-84f8-f213c1755003" class="">A civilization collapses when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-af8f-d7c786cdb6eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dS}{dt} &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-86e2-f191e6b984d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q &lt; Q_{critical}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-9abd-dab9d195770f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-8d18-d8dbb2ddbfc2" class="">Simultaneously.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ad-993b-c8b505a8a3ae" class="">Collapse is multi-variable, not single cause.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-ac89-c7de0e45b74c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-9cef-da71a85ae803" class="">VII. Invariant Law of Civilization</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-b5d9-c6a0cb3446df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{DurableCompounding} \uparrow
\iff
\psi \uparrow, Q \uparrow, V \uparrow, E \uparrow, \tau \downarrow, N \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9976-d57a452b95de" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-9329-fda0e91a030a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= extraction tax / corruption</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-afb6-c7a3907dede7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= narrative noise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b906-c8e0195f1849"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-8f62-e08fa6727609" class="">VIII. Strategic Implication</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-bc0a-cd70761e555e" class="">To win at civilizational scale:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-871f-dfeab88b2977" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Increase reinvestment efficiency</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-80e2-fdb5ee5b405a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Protect claim credibility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-9f10-c3263bbea0c3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Reduce narrative noise</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a03a-c7326acb555b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Control leverage</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-866d-e1dfc98185d8" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Maintain shock buffers</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-b6e8-eb712c85e816" class="">Money flows to systems with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-874a-eea0da830cc9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dCSI}{dt} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-9098-e76547d36387"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-a4e7-fb43bb4c1399" class="">IX. Final Structural Position</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-9782-e7c296954b68" class="">This is not political.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-91d1-cc0880330be1" class="">This is system dynamics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-9e4c-eb690cfe876e" class="">Civilizations are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-a7b2-f0314a9cd737" class="">Energy-constrained, surplus-producing, trust-dependent compounding systems subject to entropy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-83e5-e6ee4c6abd7e" class="">The scale question is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-a439-ef0b4135074d" class="">Can the system maintain positive surplus compounding under bounded entropy?</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-9643-f565ce76d844" class="">That is the entire game.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8b31-cb215dbc3422"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-80a4-c2d0817c8b23" class="">If you want next step:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-8c2c-d862c09a629f" class="">A) Convert this into investment signal engine</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-b6f1-d8c3624f9cfd" class="">B) Convert into collapse forecasting model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-9189-cd0f62306843" class="">C) Convert into country scoring dashboard</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-91e2-c81748e6077b" class="">D) Integrate into AMOS macro layer</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-866b-c506f49a8315" class="">Choose one.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-b4d4-da8085dd7723" class="">VIETNAMESE_EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-a665-d71f886c1814" class="">Dưới đây là gói <strong>triển khai đủ 4</strong> (A–D) dựa trên <strong>Civilizational Scale Framework (CSF)</strong>: (A) máy tín hiệu đầu tư, (B) mô hình dự báo sụp/khủng hoảng, (C) dashboard chấm điểm quốc gia, (D) tích hợp vào AMOS (lớp macro). Mục tiêu: <strong>ra quyết định – phân bổ vốn – cảnh báo rủi ro – ghi vết</strong> theo một “xương sống” nhất quán.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-a2a7-ec2a9c545125"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-acb3-d2167a714ab9" class="">A) Chuyển CSF thành <strong>Investment Signal Engine</strong> (tín hiệu đầu tư)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b1aa-ef58377ad2b4" class=""><strong>1) Đầu ra bắt buộc (outputs)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-accf-e728e6c01b1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>RegimeState</strong>: {Compounding / Late-cycle / Stress / Reset}</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-b370-ffa2069fef38" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CSI</strong> (Civilizational Scale Index): 0–100</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-a990-f66b1a196e35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Allocation Vector</strong>: tỷ trọng theo asset class/sector/FX duration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-b46b-d7fa5c623f91" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Risk Budget</strong>: VaR/CVaR/MaxDD mục tiêu + “hard gates” (không vượt)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-8968-fb86f1a57fdd" class=""><strong>2) Tín hiệu lõi (signal blocks)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-8456-e6d1d2039d25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>E (Energy constraint)</strong>: premium/gián đoạn nguồn cung → tác động giá &amp; biên lợi nhuận</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-9a72-d35588ba45d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>S (Surplus)</strong>: tăng trưởng thực – năng suất – tiêu dùng thiết yếu</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-96e7-d48d8a6173c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Q (Claim credibility)</strong>: rủi ro pháp lý, độ tin hợp đồng, kỷ luật tiền tệ, dòng vốn</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ae08-c5f022d99eb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>L_legit (Legitimacy/enforcement)</strong>: mức độ dự đoán được, ổn định thực thi</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-b11d-f314dbb9b2e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>T (Coherence)</strong>: phân mảnh xã hội – niềm tin – cực hóa</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-a704-edfea978c03c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R (Resilience/buffers)</strong>: dự trữ, không gian chính sách, sức chịu sốc</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9b04-eeab8d2639d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>N/B/F</strong> (Noise/Bubble/Fragmentation): tín hiệu “nhiễu hóa &amp; đòn bẩy hóa” tăng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9d00-cad0dd964118" class=""><strong>3) Luật phân bổ (deterministic controller)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-93b9-ebc72ef06bf7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exposure:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8d43-f7a216374ede" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-905b-d53342b02333" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hard gates (tự động giảm rủi ro):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-b518-dac447a26d8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Nếu hoặc → giảm leverage, tăng cash/short duration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b88a-eb8384a55583" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Nếu tăng nhanh + tăng → “anti-bubble posture” (hedge + giảm beta)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-a3ff-d6c8db234f4d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Integrity-adjusted compounding”:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-97cb-c0ee9efcc4ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a2e7-e742b6572bc0" class=""><strong>4) Deliverable</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-9012-fa92682bac4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bộ <strong>SignalSpec</strong> + <strong>Weights</strong> + <strong>Gates</strong> + <strong>Replay log</strong> (đầu vào nào → quyết định nào → lý do)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-88f6-ff45e1a9c8b4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8244-c50cb8769696" class="">B) Chuyển CSF thành <strong>Collapse / Stress Forecast Model</strong> (dự báo sụp/khủng hoảng)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8584-dca9aef36e58" class=""><strong>1) Khái niệm dự báo</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-abf8-fcc60a9b52ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Không dự báo “ngày sụp”. Dự báo <strong>hazard (xác suất có điều kiện)</strong> theo 12 tháng.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-b1aa-d192f8d70ff8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hazard tăng mạnh khi đồng thời:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-aa21-d4584f3ea0d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">, ,</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-bf05-c1415c6791b4" class=""><strong>2) Hazard function (khung chuẩn)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-8804-d997f9ca1491" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><br/>h(t) = \sigma(\beta_0 + \beta_1\Delta S + \beta_2(1-Q) + \beta_3(1-R) + \beta_4B + \beta_5N + \beta_6F)<br/></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-aa81-ed77b13544e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Output: <strong>P(stress in 3/6/12m)</strong> + <strong>Top drivers</strong> (tối đa 5)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-a8cc-ff72d2f74336" class=""><strong>3) Kịch bản stress bắt buộc</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-b327-d163697d4ae7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">5 cú sốc chuẩn: liquidity freeze, FX run, banking NPL spike, energy shock, fiscal squeeze.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-aed4-e94bd8e0347c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mỗi cú sốc có “impact map” lên + phản ứng chính sách.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-a84b-d66eca7b6fd4" class=""><strong>4) Deliverable</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-889e-fbdcf0ad6d7a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Stress Playbook</strong>: trigger → expected propagation → recommended positioning → stop rules.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-aea7-ff15227c17f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9fae-cf460a43bc8f" class="">C) <strong>Country Scoring Dashboard</strong> (bảng điểm quốc gia)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-94ab-cb350275fe86" class=""><strong>1) Thước đo (scorecard)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-8c95-c019adb58643" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CSI tổng + 6 sub-index:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-8e33-d6ff188ee845" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Energy Base (E)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-9ded-fc1497ca2060" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Surplus (S)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-82c0-d41633d8885d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Claims &amp; Contracts (Q)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-9418-e16e4344cd54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Governance Predictability (L_legit)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-bb2f-de885befcda8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Social Coherence (T)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-827f-fe93f486a2af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Buffers/Resilience (R)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-a48d-cef09000f6dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">3 “entropy penalties”: Noise (N), Bubble (B), Fragmentation (F)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-ab70-c09452474365" class=""><strong>2) Chuẩn hóa &amp; so sánh</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b8d1-efc9d52a9ef3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Score theo:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-b9fc-e394af82e98a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><strong>level</strong> (0–100)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a2b5-c846784bdf53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><strong>trend</strong> (3–6–12m)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-bc3d-cf361fe03131" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><strong>volatility</strong> (ổn định hay nhiễu)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-8c2e-d8630926164d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Traffic lights”:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-828a-f2bad38c5b5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Xanh: CSI&gt;θ và trend↑</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-88ad-c8c56f47998f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Vàng: CSI trung tính nhưng volatility↑</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9a40-eaab8c7b75d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Đỏ: CSI&lt;θ hoặc hazard↑ mạnh</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-9239-eb780d495a34" class=""><strong>3) Deliverable</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a62f-d222b68c7669" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dashboard: Country → Regime → Hazard → Allocation posture → Audit trail.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-ae99-fc6ee21194c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-979a-dfb3efbd00f3" class="">D) Tích hợp vào <strong>AMOS Macro Layer</strong> (orchestration + governance)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ad6d-f024c636a182" class=""><strong>1) Vị trí trong AMOS</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-bd16-d2e3de870a62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CSF là <strong>Macro Kernel</strong> nằm trước các engine con:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-8184-f15a1000fbdf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Intake → Normalize → CSF Score → Regime/Hazard → Policy Gate → Allocation → Execution → Audit</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-bb1d-ccda05b6e9a1" class=""><strong>2) Hợp đồng dữ liệu (schemas)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-b0e0-fb3ee03fc42a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">InputSchema (macro):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a7a5-f803ca35f2a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">{country, timestamp, signals{E,S,Q,L,T,R,N,B,F}, provenance, validity}</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a1dd-c929dfa3a959" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">OutputSchema:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-a48e-f6332c6e2de7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">{CSI, RegimeState, Hazard(3/6/12), Allocation, GatesTriggered, Explanation, AuditHash}</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-b6c6-d552b3e625da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ErrorSchema:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-a8c8-d7a7589dd4e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">{missing_data, stale_data, conflicting_signals, policy_conflict}</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9e60-e47fa50cf449" class=""><strong>3) Bảo vệ hệ thống</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-87a4-cd11622aaddd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Không cho module nào bypass:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-b8d5-f6f4c5c5c742" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">(i) Signal legitimacy (provenance/time)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-9316-f4b951548186" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">(ii) Policy &amp; risk gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-b56e-d5ea00d9f088" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">(iii) Audit ledger</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-95b3-f888c81587c5" class=""><strong>4) Deliverable</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b6fc-c4ab2429c775" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Một “macro service” duy nhất: <strong>CSF_Service</strong> + log replay deterministic.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-813f-c89f1daa9a8a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-9b62-f59b2ae53c22" class="">12 Bài Test Bắt Buộc (để 4 phần chạy “đúng hệ”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a0af-f2011cf1352a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Deterministic Replay</strong>: cùng input → cùng output (100%).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-bdc4-f00056301e77" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Missing Data</strong>: thiếu E hoặc Q → hệ giảm chức năng và báo lỗi đúng mã.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-a70e-f547237ed9a3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Stale Data</strong>: dữ liệu quá hạn → không ra allocation, chỉ ra hazard “unknown-high”.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-98bb-ceec317ac88b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Shock Injection</strong>: bơm energy shock → CSI giảm đúng hướng + gates kích hoạt.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9fe7-eacd143951aa" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>FX Run Proxy</strong>: Q giảm + R giảm → hazard tăng + giảm risk nhanh.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-a493-f883c222208b" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Bubble Build</strong>: B tăng dần → posture phòng thủ trước khi reset.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-894c-dcf0f408886b" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Policy Conflict</strong>: policy hạn chế leverage nhưng signal yêu cầu tăng → ưu tiên policy.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-84d5-f5fd0975f576" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Cross-country ranking stability</strong>: thay 1 biến nhỏ không làm đảo bảng vô lý.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-8235-d2a5171d62a2" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Regime transition</strong>: chuyển Compounding→Stress phải có điều kiện rõ ràng, không nhảy sai.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-b457-d6c43b3744cc" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>Explainability</strong>: top 5 drivers luôn khớp với biến thay đổi mạnh nhất.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-8411-c20aab6769f3" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li><strong>Backtest leakage guard</strong>: walk-forward, không lookahead.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-af56-db31524b806e" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li><strong>Kill-switch</strong>: khi volatility/hazard vượt ngưỡng → auto stop execution.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-abc3-febbb96b69e3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8abe-c33d200ac0e8" class="">Quyết định điều hành: làm gì trước để ra tiền nhanh (không vỡ hệ)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-ad87-c8847027f522"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-8372-fb199e511fa6" class="">1️⃣ Time Preference Gradient (TPG)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-ab6e-e247010c593d" class="">Most frameworks ignore this.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-b456-f6ae1b64af7c" class="">Civilizations differ in <strong>collective discount rate</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-a375-dce1bca142cf" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" TPG = E\left[\frac{1}{1+r_i}\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9092-efb6132e3ffb" class="">Where  = average social time preference.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-a31b-e520d6d2fcf2" class="">High TPG (low discount rate):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-ae4c-d03db293763a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Long-term contracts hold.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-a371-c439fc67d0cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Education compounds.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-8bd0-c2673ba2591b" class="">Low TPG (high discount rate):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a910-eb54bbdc59b4" DurableCompounding \propto \frac{1}{Average\ Discount\ Rate}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-b126-fa19069f417c" class="">This is deeper than GDP.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ba53-dac1fb5e4f47"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b7b0-e43c05a6942f" class="">2️⃣ Elite Replacement Velocity (ERV)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-ad64-c469d8ab187c" class="">Civilizations decay when elites stop being replaced by competence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-ab8c-d22bc34b39a9" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-9a90-f5f7d39707c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ERV = \frac{New\ Competent\ Elites}{Total\ Elite\ Positions}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-8aa8-fd34867f719e" class="">Low ERV:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-89f0-e144d39e5014" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stagnation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents"
