id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-98db-d6ce1c4f873c" class="">High ERV:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9eca-ccca8b85fc47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Innovation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-a167-e027e21c7048" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reset capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-bc8f-ffa19ffe0586" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Anti-fragility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-ae2c-f7f34f41ab7f" class="">Collapse probability increases when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-a9c7-cb8a8615028a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ERV \to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-b3a1-ecd76933867f" class="">This is rarely modeled.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9f62-ca81070d12eb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-9ec1-dec8e324e4aa" class="">3️⃣ Information–Reality Coupling Coefficient (IRCC)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8c0f-d15e67390d92" class="">How tightly does narrative reflect reality?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-9f32-d41a9187a887" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
IRCC = Correlation(Official\ Narrative,\ Observable\ Data)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-9305-f553e2a13a29" class="">When IRCC ↓:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8d91-e442f9765a2c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mispricing increases.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9b7b-cd54db5091e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy errors compound.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-aa5b-c8deae5a5387" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital flees silently.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-aa90-ecf9e020504b" class="">This precedes collapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-bf76-cf0b65e7192a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-9eb1-e937c7f22c5b" class="">4️⃣ Institutional Latency (IL)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-8764-e19c3892500b" class="">Speed at which system reacts to shock.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-adf3-f02a8a084a45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
IL = \frac{Time\ to\ Detect\ Shock + Time\ to\ Respond}{Shock\ Half-Life}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8592-c6a793f77cf9" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-97ed-eaf60b6de74a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
IL &gt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bb95-f43fee7ca791" class="">System reacts slower than damage propagates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-993d-eb4664158cf1" class="">That’s systemic fragility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bc7e-d35037e6295b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8476-fc1aa74c99a5" class="">5️⃣ Incentive–Capability Alignment (ICA)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-934b-e48b426f11e7" class="">Do incentives reward actual productivity?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-809c-d49b849cf38b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ICA = \frac{Return\ to\ Productive\ Activity}{Return\ to\ Rent\ Extraction}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9a08-c9225bb68424" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-ab01-f453f8ab1046" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ICA &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-8aa8-d8eef3d5deeb" class="">Rent-seeking dominates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-bbca-d5592f635997" class="">That’s late-stage extraction regime.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-8f89-e2575aaf9b03"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a1fa-f2a16b5ac86b" class="">6️⃣ Cognitive Capital Density (CCD)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-a416-c61d3e1532ca" class="">Not IQ.<br/>Not education.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-adce-d566fef47daa" class="">Actual problem-solving density per capita.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8a7a-e649b04bbd96" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CCD = \frac{High\ Skill\ Output}{Population}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-95cc-c4c6468c13bb" class="">High CCD:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-8648-d14082444600" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High innovation rate.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-9628-cf0372d3fd7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Faster adaptation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9a7c-fd8e5671170d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Faster reset recovery.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-84f5-c5f824f45679" class="">Low CCD:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-b920-dea7fe0aaace" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dependency.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-b371-c4835f302bdf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Brain drain.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-ab20-fed98f531257" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Slow repair.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-bfb5-ef0eaf345527"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a2c7-c0bb903ce48f" class="">7️⃣ Shadow Leverage Index (SLI)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-ad14-d20f7c98ac15" class="">Most systems track visible leverage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-8f47-e854d713a417" class="">Few track hidden obligations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9aad-fe492f49916e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Off-balance sheet.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-b185-ec7bf11d4245" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Political IOUs.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-b63c-ecac9354e402" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Implicit guarantees.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-bfa3-ecb4fd767fc0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SLI = Explicit\ Debt + Hidden\ Liabilities + Political\ Guarantees</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-9391-c17354cc1f5a" class="">True collapse risk is driven by SLI, not official debt.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-bc70-f037d5d790c0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-9626-f313312545a9" class="">8️⃣ Extraction Pressure Index (EPI)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-a899-d138adfb3253" class="">When surplus shrinks, extraction pressure rises.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a1c8-daf5c22a6d49" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EPI = \frac{Tax + Fees + Inflation + Regulatory\ Friction}{Real\ Surplus}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-bb25-f7619e9b2bd0" class="">High EPI:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b6d1-f04ca2df07fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital flight.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a345-d8c06f9bfb79" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Underground economy.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-9f57-e718e7ab01ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Trust decay.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-99f2-e70d3671f0f5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-a01b-e9b0a6dad40d" class="">9️⃣ Sovereign Narrative Fragility (SNF)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8a14-fe8b3931d8c9" class="">How dependent is stability on belief rather than structure?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-be75-e2f4a73b9719" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SNF = \frac{Narrative\ Dependence}{Structural\ Redundancy}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-905e-cb45127c925d" class="">High SNF:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-86f2-cc8d345295f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">One shock breaks confidence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-bb29-f65430ff1d73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">FX collapse risk increases.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ba4f-c39f5edc0aa7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-8fb1-cc0b659b8ba7" class="">🔟 Demographic Competence Skew (DCS)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-8ebe-ff88efcf1442" class="">Not just age.<br/>Skill distribution across age.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-95ff-c01e6fcbe1db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DCS = Variance(Skill\ Distribution\ Across\ Cohorts)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-87d2-e985ee60bfb1" class="">If young cohort lacks skill density:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-ad6b-e217a2daacf4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Future surplus declines.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b3e7-fbc92631b0de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pension/aging crisis intensifies.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-8315-ec0b50157d3d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-8d0a-cb03ceed3a99" class="">11️⃣ Power Centralization Elasticity (PCE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-a212-ed1f5b3cadbe" class="">How fast power concentrates under stress?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-ae14-ff4ccf327793" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PCE = \frac{\Delta Centralization}{\Delta Crisis}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8e59-fb32b05562ba" class="">High PCE:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-8b1c-dd52ce166f26" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Short-term stability.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-9134-f145f938f31e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Long-term fragility.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-ab78-c1db645f256a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-b10f-d138f59e2138" class="">12️⃣ Legitimacy Resilience Gap (LRG)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-832b-faabb646ad3c" class="">Gap between perceived legitimacy and actual performance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-ad5e-e76cc47d611d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-8f54-c0f9b8922907" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">NDF/black-market spread</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-9067-d2fb276736c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reserve drawdown / intervention intensity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9e2c-e7f6e0c1d349" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bank USD funding stress</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-bae4-ef1d0041e159" class="">2) FX hazard model (12 tháng)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-b05c-d92ac4aff857" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_{FX}(t)=\sigma(\beta_0+\beta_1\Delta Q+\beta_2\Delta R+\beta_3EPI+\beta_4SLI+\beta_5SNF+\beta_6IRCC)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-bc4c-e8e087dd6925" class="">3) “Proxy hierarchy” nếu thiếu dữ liệu (ưu tiên tín hiệu không thể che)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-afcf-e1a2a599ef9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Q (claim credibility)</strong>: CDS/sovereign spread → swap basis → NDF premium → deposit dollarization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-957e-d8416fe262fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R (buffers)</strong>: reserves/short-term debt → import cover → intervention frequency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-9f08-d1e4f368c120" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>IRCC</strong>: chênh giữa “tuyên bố” và “số không giấu được” (nhập khẩu năng lượng, PMI thực, freight, container flows)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-8ec9-cf68a6d73df5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>EPI</strong>: số lượng/chi phí thủ tục + tần suất kiểm tra + chậm hoàn/chi trả công</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8da4-ddb56533b699" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SLI</strong>: tăng trưởng tín dụng vs tăng trưởng GDP + tăng tài sản ngân hàng vs lợi nhuận thực + bùng nợ xấu “ẩn”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-b65a-efb963e302f2" class="">4) FX positioning posture theo regime</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-90a6-f39747b98a32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Compounding</strong>: carry có chọn lọc, long local assets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-99a2-f930fc17eea6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Late-cycle</strong>: giảm duration, hedge USD funding</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-83b5-d33a50c7ec31" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Stress</strong>: ưu tiên USD liquidity, tránh local convertibility risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-99ec-d2e9e5ed99df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Reset</strong>: mua tài sản “enforceable + portable” sau khi định giá sụp</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a9a9-c45ea7799800"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8f97-ca9be4f801fe" class="">C) Banking Contagion Mapping (lây lan ngân hàng)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-905a-f721fce3d5cc" class="">1) 3 tầng lây lan (MECE)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-ac31-cb72c1d2a414" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity contagion</strong>: rút tiền/không rollover → funding gap</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-b7fc-cd067068160d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Solvency contagion</strong>: nợ xấu/giảm giá tài sản → vốn âm</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-8de8-fd3f05e5c64e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Confidence contagion</strong>: tin đồn/đứt IRCC → bank run</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-98a8-fee66d718a89" class="">2) Mô hình mạng lây lan (tối giản nhưng chạy được)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-b5e9-e5635abe39b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tổn thất lan truyền:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script Capital_i - Loss_i &lt; 0 \Rightarrow Default_i=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8516-fb8a70b7aedf" class="">3) “Hidden channel” quan trọng nhất: USD funding + maturity mismatch</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-96ba-e97313ce876c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">USD funding stress proxy:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-8aa9-c8de5cae3e6e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
USDStress \approx Basis + SwapSpread + DepositDollarization</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-aa7a-f18fadd200f0" class="">4) Banking stress dashboard (tối thiểu)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-95b2-ef9b02ed102b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">NPL proxy, loan-to-deposit, liquidity coverage proxy, deposit outflow proxy, FX mismatch proxy, property collateral stress proxy.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b4f1-e2e8b482e1bc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-8864-de354c1f0aac" class="">D) Cross-Civilization Capital Migration Model (dịch chuyển vốn liên hệ)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-bcf5-f6eda52ef339" class="">1) Vốn không chạy theo “tăng trưởng”, vốn chạy theo <strong>độ cưỡng chế + khả năng thoát</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8724-ebe727ad403d" class="">Mô hình “attractor”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-a617-f688cc501b5f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Flow_{in} \propto (Enforceability \cdot Predictability \cdot Portability) - (EPI + SNF + SLI + FXHazard)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b562-ff80068164df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ACL = \frac{RegulatorySteps + ReportingBurden + ComplianceCost}{NetProductiveOutput}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-aba3-d60083cac636" class="">When ACL rises:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-9fea-cfc4ed5a93c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Small firms die first.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-af2f-dc76f7b147dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Innovation slows.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-bb60-ecc712198b9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only politically connected firms survive.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-9ffd-ddff2c4c4e59" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-abcc-f928e88b523e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\ ACL &gt; ProductivityGrowth,\ long-term stagnation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-b05d-c50ba83c68eb" class="">Overlooked because complexity looks like sophistication.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a807-fa677b95d881"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b4f2-e6e209238fb6" class="">2️⃣ Hidden Fragility of Success (HFS)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-935b-ef2c385b6f55" class="">The more stable a system appears, the more fragility it may hide.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-9bee-e2d2f6000a0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HFS \propto StabilityDuration \times LeverageDepth</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-8bd8-f5e0e0420a32" class="">Long stability + high leverage =<br/>low volatility masking deep convex risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-ba67-ff70e716a4be" class="">Markets underprice this.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-bbad-df24b76b07af"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-80f9-fdadbd310158" class="">3️⃣ Enforcement Asymmetry Index (EAI)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-ba8b-c50cb4e22569" class="">Do laws apply symmetrically?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b38a-fcadbe0c29c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EAI = \frac{EnforcementAgainstWeak}{EnforcementAgainstStrong}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b50d-c5e7c14c0f39" class="">If EAI &gt;&gt; 1:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-b213-c3a00251437b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extraction regime.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-918c-ec3b28ef46f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital exits quietly.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a512-d459e3ceed1c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Talent emigrates.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-8dbe-f1eef14e26bd" class="">This predicts brain drain before data shows it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-8c7f-d1d80d21d824"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-91c7-fd5659926339" class="">4️⃣ Informal Economy Drift (IED)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-bd0e-eaab2955afe2" class="">When trust falls, informal systems rise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-8b8d-dc9495d74321" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
IED = ShadowEconomyGrowth - FormalSectorGrowth</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-b1c8-f061ba869e72" class="">When IED &gt; 0 consistently:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-b2b6-c4bbccad6c44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tax base erodes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ae2c-fd0dce881432" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Currency weakens.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-9410-daced0353a2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy loses traction.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-bdb9-f82fcb245f2e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ac9b-cb99d71aba70" class="">5️⃣ Institutional Memory Decay (IMD)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-aac6-d37f177896f5" class="">Civilizations rely on accumulated procedural knowledge.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-964b-c306d3835c7b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
IMD = \frac{ExperiencedPersonnelExit}{TotalInstitutionalStaff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ae87-d5f2ff92b1ed" class="">When IMD spikes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9be7-eb5362e0b519" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mistakes repeat.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-9e84-cdba2a99ae71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Crisis mismanagement increases.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80de-b5f8-e0dfd3258960" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy quality drops sharply.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-938c-c5d02f39f8d2" class="">Rarely measured.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-84db-dc70fb80f210"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-8f7f-de17d825ca67" class="">6️⃣ Narrative Overextension Ratio (NOR)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-ae72-e5bbf599a886" class="">When national ambition exceeds structural capacity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b8d0-c63a98802344" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NOR = \frac{PublicAmbitionLevel}{StructuralCapacity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b4b5-cf78993e1656" class="">High NOR:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b1b8-f2b0fa1b93ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prestige projects.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-b453-ec5f055ad336" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">External conflicts.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-8bec-dca79609f23c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Internal neglect.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-9705-f378fa0f3b03" class="">Collapse risk rises if NOR sustained.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8656-c5c62183fc80"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a9ce-e2a69775fe60" class="">7️⃣ Cognitive Polarization Index (CPI)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-974e-c42d29d7052d" class="">Not political polarization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-a13b-ee2974358b9a" class="">Cognitive fragmentation — inability to share reality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-afc4-f156fca5202a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CPI = Variance(SharedFactAcceptance)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-987f-ef7958ea2e19" class="">High CPI:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-beaf-edc34774481f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy gridlock.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-9aab-fb2018a15630" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Banking panic risk.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-a66d-dda3c34715d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">FX volatility spike.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-b69a-cbe473d541d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-bb26-c988f9a871ac" class="">8️⃣ Exit Velocity Differential (EVD)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-acec-cf543b8059a6" class="">How fast capital exits vs enters.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-93c2-feef8ef8c52a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EVD = \frac{CapitalOutflowVelocity}{CapitalInflowVelocity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-a70a-d89a6d9745c1" class="">If EVD &gt; 1 persistently:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-a6f6-dd3836cc9186" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">FX pressure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9405-c67aa7823643" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Property softens.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-a12a-cd5917ab8049" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Funding spreads widen.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a5f9-ecda2b617be9" class="">Early warning before crisis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9f5b-ed46dff32bb1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-9836-cc3c10ff7489" class="">9️⃣ Silent Yield Compression (SYC)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-9e3b-f0dc5e4f5255" class="">When real returns quietly decline across sectors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-8d2d-dda7dd894989" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SYC = Decline(RealReturnAcrossIndustries)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-aa2a-f42d7e73a5c1" class="">If yields compress system-wide:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-8d5b-f8b4b520f46a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk appetite spikes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-ac10-f57e73640d9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Leverage increases.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-aaed-ca0661fdab47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bubble formation likely.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-807f-fb4b9da80c02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-848c-e6680d5351ae" class="">🔟 Sovereign Optionality Index (SOI)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9c42-e469fa75d564" class="">How many policy levers remain?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-b7a3-ecadd727f319" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SOI = MonetaryFlexibility + FiscalFlexibility + ExternalBalance + PoliticalLegitimacy</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-bed4-c4d7541d1f60" class="">Low SOI:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-80c0-c5d75a5ee39e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Crisis becomes nonlinear.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-824d-c92515c08fe1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">FX adjustment violent.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-b44f-fee7af214f98"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-b629-eb47466cf41c" class="">1️⃣1️⃣ Biological Stress Spillover (BSS)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-aa5b-f6ac58aee3b0" class="">Macro stress manifests in public health.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-8bd2-f5a945682ed8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BSS = StressMarkersPopulationWide</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-861e-fe7748f48638" class="">Rising BSS:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9536-c92163ff60ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Productivity falls.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-8c6c-ea967d05c046" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Healthcare burden rises.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-ab5e-c71146ef68af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Long-term surplus weakens.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-8cf8-ce2428791489" class="">Under-modeled but powerful.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-a215-d2502231ba90"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-aa3a-e90027305a5d" class="">1️⃣2️⃣ Elite Risk Hedging Signal (ERHS)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8a83-df0f90b98771" class="">Watch what insiders do.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9654-f82c1dc00332" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ERHS = InsiderCapitalDiversification + CitizenshipShift + AssetPortabilityShift</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-81fd-f0c7d3d62a47" class="">When consistent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-9721-ded594e12a54"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 Ottoman, USSR, Argentina, 1997 Asia, 2008 West:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-808c-fcf48d9a912a" class="">Collapse occurs when:</p></div><div href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}(Trust, Optionality, EnforcementSymmetry, EliteRenewal)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8e68-cb3e7d80d34c" class="">before consensus.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-a7ee-f68c9be0499c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b0d0-fcc839a54418" class="">If you want to go even deeper:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-a1cd-ee49a973a981" class="">A) Pre-collapse micro-signal detection in daily life</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-bbde-fe6627b69c09" class="">B) Capital flight forensic signatures</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-975e-d5f809ae041b" class="">C) FX black-market shadow metrics</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-968d-cf9d332ecae0" class="">D) 100-year cycle structural attractors</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-9e88-da024a475d56" class="">E) How AI changes collapse dynamics</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-9702-f1b57073118d" class="">Choose.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a817-de19f97e307a" class="">VIETNAMESE_EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-af59-cf6faf0f3531" class="">Dưới đây là lớp <strong>sâu hơn nữa</strong> (tầng “pre-collapse microstructure”) — các biến <strong>khó che giấu</strong>, bộc lộ qua <strong>dòng tiền vi mô, hành vi sinh tồn, cấu trúc cưỡng chế mềm</strong>, và <strong>độ lệch thực thi</strong>. Mục tiêu: tạo <strong>radar sớm</strong> + <strong>ngưỡng định lượng</strong> + <strong>điểm can thiệp còn khả thi</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-8e0e-dd430a6718e4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-a671-d8b47d85d33d" class="">1) Tầng “Cưỡng chế mềm” (Soft-Coercion Stack) — gốc của bóc tách trong pha suy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-a826-dc8b0574f04f" class=""><strong>Cưỡng chế mềm</strong> không phải bạo lực; nó là <strong>chi phí vô hình để được yên</strong>. Khi tăng, xã hội tự chuyển sang <strong>tối ưu né rủi ro</strong> thay vì tối ưu sản xuất.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-aab8-fcd1b3863e2f" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Soft Coercion Load (SCL)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-80f6-d30849bd3332" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SCL=\frac{T_{uncertainty}+C_{compliance}+C_{relationship}+C_{delay}}{DisposableSurplus}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b6b6-d418f52ca336" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: chi phí “quan hệ/giữ hòa khí/đi cửa”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bbab-f77e65ddeabe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: chi phí bị treo, bị chậm, bị giữ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-b3e1-f240e21d8ac6" class=""><strong>Ngưỡng nguy hiểm (practical):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-a1aa-ca891ab3808b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">liên tục 3–6 quý ⇒ nền kinh tế chuyển sang “trạng thái né” (avoidance regime)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-be35-f15d7e35e1c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Avoidance regime ⇒ <strong>đầu tư dài hạn giảm</strong>, <strong>đổi mới giảm</strong>, <strong>hợp đồng yếu đi</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-8ea2-f9ab65da8647"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-b1e1-e41519bc052c" class="">2) Tầng “Hợp đồng bị thay bằng tín hiệu xã hội” (Contract→Status Substitution)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-a9b3-c06e8d7fff00" class="">Khi thực thi pháp lý yếu, hệ tự chuyển sang <strong>tín hiệu địa vị</strong> để thay hợp đồng.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-9d59-f7224515049c" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Contract Substitution Ratio (CSR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-94cf-d4f29f35ea86" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CSR=\frac{Transactions_{relationship\text{-}enforced}}{Transactions_{contract\text{-}enforced}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-a2b0-e82ec6a4fe0e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">thiên kiến “ai nói” &gt; “nói gì”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-babe-df878cfa5cee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rủi ro bị chiếm đoạt tăng (capture risk)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-a2a4-c913f35bf6a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chi phí giao dịch tăng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-afbe-c3f9747593fe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-8c93-e3593750f4b3" class="">3) Tầng “Chuyển từ sản xuất sang phòng thủ” (Production→Defense Reallocation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-994f-d61e8e90e39b" class="">Dấu hiệu sâu nhất: cùng một nguồn lực, xã hội chi nhiều hơn cho <strong>phòng thủ</strong> (y tế, an ninh, kiện tụng, bảo vệ danh tiếng, dự phòng) thay vì <strong>sản xuất</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-8926-d12a619dfd50" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Defense Share (DS)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-b5cf-d2585168179f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DS=\frac{Spend_{health}+Spend_{security}+Spend_{legal}+Spend_{status}+Spend_{hedge}}{TotalSpend}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-b8b2-d2687fd3cb51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-9848-fc80f04812a6" class="">4) Tầng “Tiền tệ hai tầng” (Two-Tier Money) — dấu hiệu phá vỡ niềm tin</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-87a1-f0965c6efbc7" class="">Khi niềm tin thấp, hình thành hai tầng:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-8e5f-d2a8febcf656" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>tiền chính thức</strong> (để tuân thủ)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9646-d08240d93f55" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>tiền thực</strong> (để sinh tồn/định giá)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-b461-c9940a0c4b12" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Two-Tier Spread (TTS)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9c4d-f480c014b858" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TTS = |Price_{official} - Price_{street/proxy}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-a111-f398b3fd51a5" class="">TTS tăng ⇒ FX hazard và bank stress tăng đồng thời.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-a452-facf49ff6a1b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-8593-ddd9945556ff" class="">5) Tầng “Hệ thống trì hoãn có chủ đích” (Intentional Delay Regime)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-8fef-e34d58e08242" class="">Khi thiếu tiền, hệ dùng trì hoãn để “vay không lãi” từ dân/doanh nghiệp.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-9c23-e8071c6038aa" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Payment Delay Index (PDI)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-84e3-f8a9645cb827" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PDI=\frac{AvgDays_{payables}}{AvgDays_{receivables}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-b61b-e1afa68f34ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8f67-e72f078c77ad" class="">6) Tầng “Chất lượng thực thi bất đối xứng” (Enforcement Asymmetry) — lõi của mất niềm tin</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-b9b8-e3dcb0760f6a" class="">Bạn đã chạm đúng: bất đối xứng thực thi làm gãy đạo đức kinh tế.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-bd7f-d055322f4fc0" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Enforcement Asymmetry Index (EAI)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-842c-e525d264ffe2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EAI=\frac{EnforcementRate_{weak}}{EnforcementRate_{strong}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-a6ca-e8e5240b56f5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9021-ec982be5ac3b" class="">7) Tầng “Sức khỏe dân số như một chỉ báo vĩ mô” (Biological Macro Signal)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-810e-f78487ac4e93" class="">Khi stress kéo dài, năng suất tương lai giảm trước khi GDP phản ánh.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-9f45-e41df102a7b8" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Biological Stress Spillover (BSS)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-b1bc-f3b21ed39c99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BSS=\Delta(ER\ visits,\ sick\ leave,\ insomnia,\ hypertension,\ digestive\ disorders)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-aa94-c08712daa514"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-b1dc-c0de3d547204" class="">8) Tầng “Chỉ báo không thể giấu”: Hành vi tiêu dùng lệch chuẩn</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b3a1-c4b3b4f221cb" class="">Các món “khó fake”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-8d40-c4e0c747cba5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chi cho <strong>tâm linh/lottery/giải hạn</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-9d38-fc77b48e1e2c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chi cho <strong>trốn né</strong> (dịch vụ giấy tờ, môi giới)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-8b3f-f3af18cb8221" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chi cho <strong>đặt cọc an toàn</strong> (ngoại tệ, vàng, tài sản portable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8840-e86cb1bd6a0c" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Escape Spend Ratio (ESR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-bdae-ee06434e28bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ESR=\frac{Spend_{portable}+Spend_{escape}+Spend_{hedge}}{Spend_{productive}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8625-f0fbc31a85de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-9efc-cf2d37b48a08" class="">9) AI làm gì khác: Tăng tốc “Goodhart hóa” và tăng độ dốc sụp (collapse slope)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b9a3-c32922ea3316" class="">AI không tạo khủng hoảng một mình; AI <strong>làm 3 thứ</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-973e-f176705693e4" class="">(i) Tăng tốc Goodhart signals</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a7a0-ef2d8db972d0" class="">Khi hệ dùng tín hiệu để kiểm soát, tín hiệu mất thông tin. AI làm điều này nhanh hơn vì:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-8737-ed079035b6fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tối ưu hóa trên KPI ngay tức thì</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-99bc-f4f3e4e41a84" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tạo “hành vi diễn” quy mô lớn</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-a206-ee1aaa83ad55" class=""><strong>Chỉ số: Signal Decay Rate (SDR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a6ce-feef16501100" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SDR=\frac{d}{dt}\Big(Corr(KPI,\ RealOutcome)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-a40d-e019daac48d4" class="">(ii) Tăng bất đối xứng thông tin (information weaponization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-a5c6-e31f8ef8948d" class="">AI làm “lời nói” rẻ hơn, “bằng chứng” nhiễu hơn ⇒ IRCC giảm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-9750-d15b9c517719" class="">(iii) Tăng tốc vòng phản hồi tiêu cực (negative feedback loop speed)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-be52-d13b32f64286" class="">Chu kỳ từ “tin đồn → rút tiền → siết thanh khoản” diễn ra nhanh hơn.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9b06-f725443ac717"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-93eb-dfb1252c95a6" class="">10) Ngưỡng “irreversible vs reversible” (tầng sâu)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-842c-c50d684ab7c3" class="">A) Có thể đảo (reversible) nếu can thiệp sớm</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-ba61-f337657a0070" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ACL/SCL tăng vừa phải</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-93c4-dfbab01df684" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PDI tăng nhưng chưa lan hệ thống</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-af33-c2b2be3e1100" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">IRCC giảm nhưng chưa đứt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-9444-dd29998b397e" class="">B) Gần như không đảo (quasi-irreversible) khi đạt cụm ngưỡng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-b3b4-c18d78327605" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CSR cao (hợp đồng bị thay bởi quan hệ) + EAI cao</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-bec9-f9b3f2991456" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Two-tier money (TTS) hình thành ổn định</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-b0b9-fa0e9caf1c8e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">IMD (mất trí nhớ thể chế) tăng do người giỏi rời đi</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-a76c-cb749ed3e9b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ESR tăng mạnh (xã hội chuyển sang “thoát”)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-bcfe-cb4e2154c6a1" class=""><strong>Điều kiện cụm (cluster)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-8812-f9e880b72f84" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(EAI\uparrow \land CSR\uparrow \land TTS\uparrow) \Rightarrow ExitVelocity\uparrow \Rightarrow IRCC\downarrow\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-82db-c0c7e1076a65"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-baf3-c22dd5f1d2c6" class="">11) “Radar sớm” dùng 12 biến khó che (khuyến nghị)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-adf3-fc99cbac870f" class="">Chỉ cần 12 biến để phát hiện pha suy trước số liệu:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-a7e1-d3a0b1df91eb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>SCL</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-8fa4-d48fbcba3e07" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>CSR</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a7ef-f90c90a79ebc" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>DS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-94aa-e5ba938c4186" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>TTS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-a2e1-e6c5ff8d5a1b" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>PDI</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-b7b4-e7957d4633fa" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>EAI</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-867d-db631eee04e8" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>ESR</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-854e-e248b64df5ca" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>IMD proxy (turnover nhân sự chủ chốt)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-82f6-c515157c271e" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Deposit dollarization proxy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a6a0-e23dc97e592b" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>SME closure rate (phi chính thức cũng được)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-9f87-dd2d3ff913f7" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li>Loan rollover friction (khó gia hạn)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-a197-d6f7294b2ecb" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li>Complaint intensity (tần suất/độ gay gắt khi nói về tiền–nợ–bệnh)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-874f-fb2c84d32ec9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-8705-c4ffd25fe7b8" class="">12) Kết luận điều hành</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-add7-c95beca39a56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Sụp” trong xã hội hiện đại thường là <strong>mục ruỗng chức năng</strong> trước khi sụp bề mặt.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-ac55-dab0478b90f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cái chết của hệ bắt đầu khi: <strong>hợp đồng mất giá</strong>, <strong>thực thi bất đối xứng</strong>, <strong>tiền hai tầng</strong>, <strong>chi phí cưỡng chế mềm tăng</strong>, và <strong>dân số ăn vốn sinh học</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-90f1-ddb3c541fba3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI làm pha này <strong>nhanh hơn</strong> vì tăng Goodhart hóa và giảm IRCC.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-a112-d59be51fca41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-b8c9-dd79a5386e8e" class="">Nếu bạn muốn đi <strong>sâu hơn nữa</strong>, nhánh tiếp theo (chọn 1):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a6e6-ec4e9020ecb1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Bộ ngưỡng định lượng 12 tháng</strong>: FX hazard + bank stress (gắn vào dashboard)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-b34e-cb53372c5a29" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Mô hình “collapse slope”</strong>: tốc độ suy vs khả năng phục hồi (resilience)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-aabe-fdb895dcc7c8" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Bộ chỉ báo đời sống Việt Nam</strong>: proxy cụ thể theo ngữ cảnh VN (không dùng dữ liệu “trên giấy”)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-b33d-cb92003a8aad" class="">VIETNAMESE_EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY<br/>Bạn yêu cầu “More” và “Eng”, nhưng hệ trả lời của tôi bắt buộc chỉ xuất 1 lớp tóm tắt điều hành bằng tiếng Việt. Dưới đây là tầng sâu hơn (ít người mô hình hóa) theo hướng định lượng – khó che giấu – chạy được.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-9500-c7b9a67e7f74" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Tầng “Micro-price truth” (sự thật giá vi mô) — dữ liệu không thể bẻ cong lâu<br/>Ý tưởng: Khi “số trên giấy” sai, giá vi mô và ma sát giao dịch sẽ lộ trước.<br/>Bộ chỉ số<br/>Friction Inflation (FI):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-be29-ce3c1b137f6f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Tầng “Working Capital Blood Loss” (rút máu vốn lưu động) — cách hệ sống bằng trì hoãn<br/>Dấu hiệu: nền thiếu tiền mặt sẽ biến doanh nghiệp thành “ngân hàng bất đắc dĩ”.<br/>Net Working Capital Squeeze (NWCS):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-abd7-f0114de3b7f2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Tầng “Informal Credit Replaces Banks” (tín dụng phi chính thức thay ngân hàng)<br/>Khi tín dụng chính thức co lại, tín dụng quan hệ phình lên.<br/>Shadow Credit Share (SCS):<br/>Ngưỡng: SCS↑ bền vững ⇒ hệ rơi vào “tín dụng kém chất lượng” ⇒ rủi ro FX/bank tăng.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-ab3e-feb288647e52" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Tầng “Two-tier enforcement” (thực thi hai tầng) — lõi làm gãy đạo đức kinh tế<br/>Không cần tranh luận đạo đức;
