chỉ cần đo bất đối xứng thực thi.<br/>Enforcement Asymmetry Index (EAI):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-ac21-c1e4414121b2" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Tầng “Sovereign balance-sheet hiddenness” (bảng cân đối nhà nước ẩn)<br/>Cú sốc thường đến từ “nợ/chi ngoài sổ”.<br/>Off-balance Burden Proxy (OBB):<br/>Ngưỡng: OBB↑ + SOI↓ (tùy chọn chính sách giảm) ⇒ hazard tăng phi tuyến.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-b230-c06472ad2996" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Tầng “Health-to-productivity collapse” (ăn vốn sinh học)<br/>Bạn đã chạm đúng: xã hội có thể “chạy” bằng thân thể dân một thời gian, rồi gãy.<br/>Biological Stress Spillover (BSS):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-87ae-de80ef725afc" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Tầng “Elite exit without headlines” (giới tinh hoa rút khỏi hệ)<br/>Không nhìn lời nói; nhìn cấu trúc tài sản và tính di động.<br/>Elite Hedge Signal (EHS):<br/>Ngưỡng: EHS↑ bền vững ⇒ xác suất “đổ dốc” tăng.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-93c1-e123c462d89a" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Tầng “AI-accelerated Goodhart collapse” (AI làm hỏng thước đo nhanh hơn)<br/>Khi KPI được dùng làm roi, KPI mất nghĩa; AI làm tốc độ này tăng mạnh.<br/>Signal Decay Rate (SDR):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-8986-d0d559c6a046" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>“Collapse slope” (độ dốc sụp) — thứ bạn đang cảm nhận là “mọi thứ mục từ trong”<br/>Không phải “sụp hay không”, mà là tốc độ suy chức năng.<br/>Collapse Slope (CS):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-84a1-ecff9020c6b2" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Bộ 15 proxy “đời sống” (VN-style, khó che) để bạn tự quan sát<br/>DSO/DPO thực tế trong SME<br/>Tỷ lệ trả hàng/đổi trả tăng<br/>Thời gian giải quyết thủ tục kéo dài<br/>Phí “dịch vụ giấy tờ” tăng<br/>Dollarization ngầm<br/>NDF/basis/chi phí chuyển tiền (nếu tiếp cận được)<br/>Cửa hàng đóng/đổi ngành nhanh<br/>Cầm đồ/vay nóng lan rộng<br/>Chi cho tâm linh/lottery tăng<br/>Chi cho y tế/đồ bổ/thuốc tăng<br/>Mức độ cáu bẳn về tiền–nợ–bệnh trong hội thoại<br/>Người giỏi rời khỏi tổ chức (turnover key staff)<br/>Giá thuê mặt bằng giảm nhưng vẫn ế<br/>“Giảm giá sâu” trở thành bình thường<br/>Trễ thanh toán trong chuỗi cung ứng<br/>Quyết định điều hành<br/>Muốn “đọc sự thật” trong môi trường thiếu minh bạch: đo FI, NWCS, TTS, EAI, BSS, EHS, SDR.<br/>Đây là các biến **</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-9949-cecbe8312d06" class="">VIETNAMESE_EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8491-fe9dac7addd6" class="">Dưới đây là 2 phần bạn yêu cầu: <strong>(A) Bộ chỉ báo đời sống VN – proxy “không trên giấy”</strong> và <strong>(B) Mô hình “collapse slope” (tốc độ suy) vs “resilience” (khả năng phục hồi)</strong>. Thiết kế để <strong>quan sát thực địa</strong>, <strong>định lượng được</strong>, <strong>ghép thành dashboard</strong>, <strong>chạy được</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bbf7-fb73a7999417"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9672-cffefe011138" class="">A) Bộ chỉ báo đời sống Việt Nam (proxy theo ngữ cảnh VN, không dùng dữ liệu công bố)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-aa1c-c11a59318ce4" class="">A1) Cụm “Tiền–Nợ–Thanh khoản” (khó che nhất)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8e2d-ebf1c40755e5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Độ trễ thanh toán dây chuyền (Chain Payment Delay, CPD)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a85d-f5253ff5bdce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: “khất”, “đảo”, “bù trừ”, “gối đầu” lan theo chuỗi cung ứng.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-99a6-cddd4b91d282" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CPD=\text{median}(\Delta days\_to\_pay) \ \text{(qua 20–50 doanh nghiệp/đầu mối)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-afa7-e87b134d16e5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Độ khó vay/đảo nợ thực tế (Rollover Friction, RF)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-85b0-f476c15cba87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: “đòi thêm tài sản”, “cắt hạn mức”, “đẩy phí ngoài”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-b36c-fca176092e09" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RF = w_1\Delta CollateralReq + w_2\Delta Fees + w_3\Delta TimeToApproval</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-b778-c82dd863abf1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cầm đồ/vay nóng &amp; phí vốn phi chính thức (Shadow Cost of Capital, SCC)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-8edb-d349f42e0e92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SCC=\text{median}(rate_{informal})-\text{median}(rate_{formal})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-ad3a-fe3ca9792ec9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tín hiệu “tiền hai tầng” (Two-tier Money, TTM)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9e36-dfa5c2aabc6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: chênh giá hàng nhập, phụ phí chuyển tiền, phí logistics “mềm”, chênh giá vàng/FX “thực dụng”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-9f60-df8664c98809" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TTM=\sum_k |Price^{street}_k-Price^{official}_k|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-be21-da459f043724"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9d65-f96b05f89c7e" class="">A2) Cụm “Giá–Giỏ hàng–Sức mua” (lộ qua hành vi)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-99de-c94a0fbbf4dc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Giỏ hàng co lại + trading down (Basket Compression, BC)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-86b7-cab90c31787a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: chuyển sang hàng rẻ, giảm đạm, giảm hàng “thưởng”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-83f4-ded672f65230" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BC=\Delta Share_{low\_tier} - \Delta Share_{mid/high}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-8f3f-d7ff6f856e77" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tần suất khuyến mãi sâu trở thành bình thường (Discount Normalization, DN)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-95df-fa096c1d773b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DN=\frac{\#SKU\_discount&gt;30\%}{\#SKU\_total}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-ac34-f272e8dabe9c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tỷ lệ trả hàng/đổi trả/hoàn tiền (Return Stress, RS)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9611-df3a74815fca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RS=\frac{Returns+RefundRequests}{Orders}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9abf-c178fec1f376"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8753-fedd3e5268b8" class="">A3) Cụm “Việc làm–Thu nhập–Tâm thế” (đo bằng hành vi, không cần số)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b45d-e92ad499764f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tăng làm thêm / chạy gig / bán lẻ nhỏ (Hustle Spike, HS)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-b8fc-f5279f785968" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: người “làm 2–3 việc”, bán hàng lặt vặt, dịch vụ thời vụ.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-8310-fa9859666c9c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HS=\Delta \text{(tần suất hành vi kiếm thêm trong mẫu quan sát)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-a4b5-d77c1cf20fe1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Mật độ “than về tiền, nợ, bệnh” trong hội thoại (Complaint Intensity, CI)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-afaf-d0200f680fe5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dùng mẫu quan sát: nhóm chat, quán cà phê, tài xế, shop.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8e32-c17bedc60f4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CI=\frac{\#mentions(\text{tiền/nợ/bệnh})}{\#total\_mentions}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-9fbc-d3fa4ac5561e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Xu hướng “sợ cam kết dài” (Long-horizon Aversion, LHA)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-ae36-dac4415dde9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: thuê ngắn hạn, hợp đồng ngắn, ngại đầu tư.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-8a66-c073b3330c28" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
LHA=\Delta Share_{short\_term\_choices}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-b801-d7a034eb4760"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-b460-e2d0159e29e5" class="">A4) Cụm “Sức khỏe–Sinh học” (tín hiệu không thể giả lâu)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-8dc8-fd48d973f52b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tăng rối loạn tiêu hóa, mất ngủ, tăng huyết áp, suy nhược (Bio-stress, BSS)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9317-ed1a343d09a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: đơn giản nhất là tần suất kể + nhu cầu thuốc/khám.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-804e-e2444b4c50d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BSS=\Delta f(GI)+\Delta f(insomnia)+\Delta f(HTN)+\Delta f(fatigue)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-96b4-c823dddea83c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Chi tiêu “phòng thủ” tăng (Defense Spend, DS)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-81f3-f57c43e00a0e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: vitamin/thuốc, khám tư, bảo vệ/giữ xe, dịch vụ giấy tờ, “an toàn”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a499-f27e88100116" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DS=\frac{Spend_{health+security+paperwork}}{TotalSpend}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-a0ce-ed461c79ad31"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-9405-fd171dfdd158" class="">A5) Cụm “Thể chế–Thực thi–Quan hệ” (cốt lõi của mất niềm tin)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-b7f4-d7eeaca12f7a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Chi phí quan hệ/giấy tờ tăng (Soft Coercion Load, SCL)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-a9a7-e5bee7490a2e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SCL=\frac{C_{relationship}+C_{paperwork}+C_{delay}}{DisposableSurplus}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-b72e-e310ba5ad00e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Độ lệch thực thi (Enforcement Asymmetry, EAI)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8694-cb641391d207" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proxy: “ai bị làm khó, ai được bỏ qua”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-af91-ce9c76fba976" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EAI=\frac{Enforcement_{weak}}{Enforcement_{strong}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-aa88-d3056f547688" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tỷ lệ giao dịch “nói miệng” tăng (Contract Substitution, CSR)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bf3f-fac7e923b556" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CSR=\frac{Deals_{relationship\text{-}enforced}}{Deals_{contract\text{-}enforced}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-a2f2-e92327bc73af"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-a432-d3f87d361d47" class="">A6) Cụm “Thoát hệ” (exit)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9a09-c6176df75b6b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Nhu cầu vàng/FX/tài sản portable (Portable Demand, PD)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-be91-f5d11f4e89de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PD=\Delta Share_{portable\ assets}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a2fc-e2a7b1d4d229" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Nhu cầu định cư/du học/hộ chiếu (Exit Intent, EI)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-b29b-f44fa29667c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EI=\Delta f(\text{định cư/du học/visa})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-bdaf-e63cdeb6a3f1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tín hiệu “giữ tiền mặt, không mở rộng” (Cash Hoarding, CH)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-8a0d-c1ae1b457815" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CH=\Delta Share_{cash\ preference}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-aaee-ec495f3e8390"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bc71-c919a799fa55" class="">A7) Cụm “Tâm linh–Cờ bạc–Hy vọng thay thế” (proxy của tuyệt vọng)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-bb21-d7d7a2715b39" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Chi cho tâm linh/giải hạn/lottery tăng (Hope Substitution, HSI)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-b4fa-c7bd227819ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HSI=\frac{Spend_{spiritual+lottery}}{Spend_{productive}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-91d8-e7ab69063b1a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Ngôn ngữ “đành chịu/đứt rồi/không tin ai” tăng (Trust Collapse Language, TCL)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-9be7-f4c0e255edf8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TCL=\Delta f(\text{cụm từ tuyệt vọng/hoài nghi})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-b11a-e83ee5ac9bae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-b318-c6805fad722c" class="">B) Mô hình “Collapse Slope” vs “Resilience” (tốc độ suy và khả năng phục hồi)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-a086-ee9d5c245995" class="">B1) Định nghĩa 2 đại lượng cốt lõi</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-8ef3-c662ebd50773" class="">1) Collapse Slope (CS) – độ dốc suy chức năng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-9bc9-d389af7eb1f2" class="">Đo tốc độ xấu đi của các proxy “khó che”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a797-d410052c24af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CS(t)=\frac{d}{dt}\Big(
z(CPD)+z(RF)+z(SCC)+z(TTM)+z(SCL)+z(EAI)+z(BSS)+z(HSI)
\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a764-f009014ab0d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CS cao nghĩa là “xấu nhanh”, không cần GDP.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-bd66-cd12d884cda9" class="">2) Resilience (R) – khả năng hấp thụ và hồi phục</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-80f9-cf7696239d14" class="">Đo “đệm” của hệ: nguồn lực, linh hoạt, niềm tin, năng lực sửa lỗi.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-a762-f6ed19102eb5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R(t)=
w_1 z(SOI)+w_2 z(IRCC)+w_3 z(Slack)+w_4 z(EnforcementSymmetry)+w_5 z(SocialSupport)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-9119-c2599dab8422" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Slack</strong>: dư địa vốn lưu động, dự phòng, khả năng “chịu 3 tháng không chết”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-8a43-e8203a7c18fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>IRCC (Integrity-Repair Capacity)</strong>: khả năng sửa sai minh bạch (được dân tin).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-80f3-e52fdeba3ce8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>EnforcementSymmetry</strong>: luật áp đều.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-93e8-d34dc0c3e5f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SocialSupport</strong>: mạng hỗ trợ gia đình/cộng đồng.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-9159-e865de686a60"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80de-b738-cdd887d42ca1" class="">B2) “Ngưỡng chuyển pha” (phase transition) — khi nào từ xấu thành gãy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-83c9-ddd9743253fb" class="">Điều kiện gãy (Break Condition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-9ab9-f566d6f20c7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Break} \iff CS&gt; \theta_{CS} \ \land \ R&lt;\theta_R \ \text{liên tục } k \text{ tuần}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-aa50-c1055f523449" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">đến tuần cho tín hiệu đời sống.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-80c2-f818b5dbae03" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: top 80–90 percentile lịch sử nội bộ (baseline bạn tự dựng).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-9f25-fbea569976ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: bottom 10–20 percentile.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-80cb-e5ea8b054bf9" class="">“Tốc độ sụp” khi đã vượt ngưỡng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8b15-e7d3de574a80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Hazard_{collapse}(t)=\sigma\big(\alpha\cdot CS(t)-\beta\cdot R(t)+\gamma\cdot Shock(t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-b1a5-c60e8e5f98ca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b959-f882cdafa463" class="">B3) Cách dùng mô hình trong thực tế (không cần dữ liệu nhà nước)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-89f9-fe8d261ec41a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Lấy mẫu 50–200 điểm quan sát (doanh nghiệp nhỏ, logistic, bán lẻ, y tế, dịch vụ giấy tờ).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-8f3c-e1083f00ae53" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Gán điểm 0–5 cho mỗi proxy mỗi tuần.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-a2d4-f9018ae4a72b" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Tính CS(t) và R(t).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8ee7-c5fa1d6752a5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Báo động khi Break Condition kích hoạt.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-a810-cba0d8329ee1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-850f-cf2108a663db" class="">B4) “Điểm can thiệp còn khả thi” (levers)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-aef6-dee5fca41649" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nếu <strong>CS tăng vì CPD/RF/SCC</strong>: can thiệp thanh khoản chuỗi cung ứng (không phải bơm vĩ mô chung).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-b67a-f85504bce599" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nếu <strong>CS tăng vì EAI/SCL/CSR</strong>: can thiệp thực thi + giảm chi phí thủ tục (điểm nghẽn thể chế).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-8ba7-f4713b8bf7fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nếu <strong>CS tăng vì BSS/DS</strong>: can thiệp y tế cơ bản + giảm stress vận hành (bảo toàn “vốn sinh học”).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-9abd-e0acf9610d7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nếu <strong>HSI/TCL tăng</strong>: xã hội đã vào “thoát hệ” — lúc này chỉ còn tác dụng nếu phục hồi niềm tin thực thi.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-bbd3-fee9dae197cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-be4a-df95922b656c" class="">Kết luận điều hành</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-a926-c32f8b874b8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bộ proxy trên cho phép bạn <strong>đọc VN như một hệ thống thiếu minh bạch</strong> bằng tín hiệu <strong>không che được</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-b4d1-d78d71f0bab6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mô hình CS vs R cho bạn <strong>một đồng hồ đo</strong>: “xấu nhanh đến đâu” và “còn đệm để cứu không”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-85c8-eaf135dea7c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nếu bạn muốn đóng gói thành sản phẩm: dashboard 20 proxy + CS/R + cảnh báo 6–12 tuần.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-806d-db7ec1956d56" class="">Nếu bạn muốn, bước tiếp theo tôi sẽ chốt:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8b71-fbb9598eaf60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>thang điểm 0–5 cho từng proxy</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-a7d2-e3b989e4adaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>template khảo sát 10 phút/tuần</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8fed-c5876257ecac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>cách chuẩn hóa baseline</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b54a-fc120987f36b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">và <strong>ngưỡng cảnh báo 3 cấp</strong> (Watch / Warning / Break).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-ae5d-de6aa3dae8ca" class="">VIETNAMESE_EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-95c4-c6b4ac9031ec" class="">Bạn muốn “overlooked + deeper” theo ngữ cảnh VN (không dùng số liệu công bố). Dưới đây là <strong>8 tầng sâu hơn</strong> mà đa số phân tích bỏ sót, kèm <strong>proxy đo được</strong>, <strong>equations</strong>, và <strong>ngưỡng chuyển pha</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-9686-f02c5289a7c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a5b7-e5b7954a82b4" class="">1) Tầng “Thể chế vận hành thật” (Operating State Capacity, không phải luật trên giấy)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8c92-f7cd89177a27" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> VN vận hành theo <strong>khả năng thực thi</strong> và <strong>băng thông xử lý hồ sơ</strong> chứ không theo quy định.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-80e3-d05435a6db9d" class="">Proxy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-b309-dfebdf20759f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Time-to-Closure (TTC)</strong>: thời gian khép hồ sơ (đăng ký/thuế/đất/hoàn thuế/giấy phép).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-b17c-d47c4b79b732" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Rework Loop Rate (RLR)</strong>: tỷ lệ “làm lại/đi lại”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-944b-c661ea9c201d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RLR=\frac{\#rework\_cycles}{\#cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-b48f-e992dd6962cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DL=\frac{\#cases\_require\_human\_discretion}{\#cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-bbce-c80bdbb8eb41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-9c78-fbd819919e88" class="">2) Tầng “Kinh tế phong bì” như một sắc thuế ẩn (Soft Tax, không ghi nhận)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-9bc6-e2885dc42013" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> chi phí mềm tăng là dấu hiệu nhà nước/đơn vị thiếu ngân sách và doanh nghiệp thiếu lối ra.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-ad0f-e0f7a003009b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SoftTaxRate=\frac{C_{paperwork}+C_{relationship}+C_{delay}}{GrossMargin}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-82f3-fb954e90da35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">phí dịch vụ giấy tờ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b814-e531e7514781" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“chi phí bôi trơn” (bất kể gọi tên gì)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-8c5a-fcbb34938e78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">thời gian chết của chủ doanh nghiệp</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-a015-ca5497ef2c7c" class=""><strong>Ngưỡng:</strong> SoftTaxRate vượt biên lợi nhuận ⇒ SME “tắt máy” (đóng cửa/đi ngầm/giảm chất lượng).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-8b37-ee1cbfeb5665"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8a0b-df0da339bbc7" class="">3) Tầng “Chuỗi thanh toán → chuỗi đạo đức” (Payment Ethics Cascade)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-9a3e-f144db032766" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> khi <strong>không trả đúng hạn</strong> thành mặc định, xã hội chuyển từ “niềm tin” sang “phòng thủ”, kéo theo gian dối.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-8bad-e1fd7cc96016" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EthicsCascade = f(CPD,\,SCC,\,EAI)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a969-f47d0c218a79" class=""><strong>Proxy:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a67a-f8b435d00159" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">điều khoản trả chậm lan rộng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-922c-c36c5305328c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">đòi cọc/đòi giữ hàng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-abc0-db69c24ffc51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“bẻ kèo” tăng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-b02d-ca0361382ef1" class=""><strong>Ngưỡng:</strong> CPD↑ mạnh + SCC↑ ⇒ gian lận hợp đồng tăng là hệ quả cơ học, không phải “tính cách dân”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-89da-fbb029776b2b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-84c8-f819b54e4389" class="">4) Tầng “Thị trường lao động thật: kiệt lực + rút năng lực” (Human Capital Drain)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9797-ddb4dcc1b3a0" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> VN không thiếu người, thiếu <strong>người còn sức và còn tin</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b85d-fb0802bc5a51" class="">Proxy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8178-d3dc82a124c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Key Staff Half-life (KSH)</strong>: thời gian giữ nhân sự chủ chốt.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-96b2-da3afee8b8fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
KSH=\text{median}(tenure_{key})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-96d1-e2bb7068d264" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cognitive Bandwidth Loss (CBL)</strong>: mức giảm năng lực tập trung do stress.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-ade7-f2776ee3874c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CBL \propto BSS + DS</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801a-9ec0-ff5ef6532c7b" class=""><strong>Ngưỡng:</strong> KSH↓ + QQD↑ + BSS↑ ⇒ năng suất “rụng từ lõi”, GDP vẫn có thể đẹp.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-b19e-df763a8bcca2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-9a4d-f2a20c9df5c2" class="">5) Tầng “Bong bóng tài sản không cần tăng giá” (Balance-sheet Illusion)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-90b2-d1251c4c4d29" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> gãy không đến từ giá giảm ngay, mà từ <strong>không bán được</strong> + <strong>không xoay được</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9c2c-df7ef8db32a1" class="">Proxy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-8412-e0e7074fe32b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Liquidity Freeze (LF)</strong>: thời gian bán tăng, giao dịch thật giảm.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-94b0-fca330b277e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
LF=\Delta DaysOnMarket + \Delta \frac{Listings}{Transactions}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-85fb-c0e0a008afc9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CHC=\Delta(Valuation\_ratio)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-861f-f4361fffbe0a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-b6c0-c15cd0debf6a" class="">6) Tầng “FX không nổ bằng tỷ giá – nổ bằng kênh chuyển đổi” (Convertibility Stress)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-a430-d3ced8ef3069" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> ở môi trường kiểm soát, FX stress lộ qua <strong>độ khó chuyển</strong>, <strong>phí</strong>, <strong>độ trễ</strong>, <strong>hạn mức</strong>, không nhất thiết qua giá niêm yết.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-931b-dcc75636d1d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FXFriction = \Delta Time + \Delta Fee + \Delta LimitTightness</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-b5c9-ebcc9bb90b3a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">doanh nghiệp “khó mua ngoại tệ”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-83ed-f361d8cafa0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tăng giấy tờ chứng minh</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9082-eb3cae3bbd73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“chuyển tiền chậm/đợi duyệt”<br/><strong>Ngưỡng:</strong> FXFriction↑ bền vững ⇒ hazard FX/bank tăng phi tuyến.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-8416-c84b10dfc765"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-9824-cf1e3bcf139c" class="">7) Tầng “Truyền thông xã hội = cảm biến suy thoái” (Social Exhaust Signal)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a6ec-f145fca12635" class=""><strong>Ý tưởng:</strong> VN che được báo cáo; khó che được <strong>ngôn ngữ đời sống</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-8564-f448d1638448" class="">Proxy đo nhanh</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-92c0-de5bfee609cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>TCL</strong> (Trust Collapse Language): “không tin ai”, “toang”, “hết đường”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-bd07-d7b5f379bdf6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>HSI</strong> (Hope Substitution Index): giải hạn, bói toán, lottery tăng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bcab-fea23e007e57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HSI=\frac{Spend_{spiritual+lottery}}{Spend_{productive}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-ad6f-c3eb7aee0459"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-a4ba-db518787f15e" class="">8) Tầng “Điểm không hồi phục” (Irreversibility Map)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-bfe7-f1c44f0a9056" class="">Bạn cần phân loại cái gì <strong>đảo được</strong> và <strong>khó đảo</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-af2d-c169c1223428" class="">8.1 Đảo được (nếu can thiệp đúng điểm)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-b92a-e0d50880835b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">thanh khoản chuỗi cung ứng (CPD/RF/SCC)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-a46d-e94a27665b79" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">thủ tục hành chính (TTC/RLR)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-885f-ca0d32d17dd2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chi phí mềm (SoftTaxRate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ba82-c8b708141165" class="">8.2 Khó đảo (vì dính niềm tin và vốn sinh học)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-9ee5-e30ef18c8c17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">KSH/CBL: “người giỏi + người còn sức” đã rời</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-8ea6-eaba7b4ffdd5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">TCL: “mất niềm tin” lan</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-8f3f-d7599fb9b969" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DS/BSS: tích lũy bệnh nền, suy thần kinh</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8341-f43f11724e07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EAI kéo dài: làm hỏng kỳ vọng công bằng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-be17-c8eb9e76f431"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-b768-c25d52c69249" class="">Mô hình sâu hơn: “Collapse Slope” có thêm “Resilience Depletion”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a6a2-fc2f7be5c738" class="">Bạn đã có CS(t). Bổ sung <strong>Resilience Depletion Rate (RDR)</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-bc1c-cec25f1326c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RDR(t)= -\frac{d}{dt}R(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-aace-e567b31d4a69" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Hazard(t)=\sigma(\alpha CS(t)+\beta RDR(t)+\gamma Shock(t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-ad56-fb45099a8138"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-8704-d8f18c357fb1" class="">Quyết định điều hành</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-b2fd-ca5c2a21ae20" class="">Nếu bạn muốn “deeper” để dùng ngay, 3 gói mà thị trường VN thiếu nhất:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-bd30-cb919591b403" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Convertibility Stress Radar (FXFriction + CPD + CHC)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-94d9-cc236517a080" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Human Capital Drain Radar (KSH + CBL + BSS + QQD)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-9530-f2c4ccdfe541" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Soft Tax &amp; Enforcement Dashboard (SoftTaxRate + EAI + TTC + RLR)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-a94d-c6ead0f4728e" class="">Chọn 1 gói, tôi sẽ chốt <strong>bộ câu hỏi lấy mẫu 15 phút</strong>, <strong>thang điểm</strong>, <strong>công thức tổng</strong>, và <strong>ngưỡng cảnh báo 3 mức</strong> để bạn triển khai như sản phẩm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-b32e-d5e2c5322d3b" class="">VIETNAMESE_EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a51b-d88c0bff1bf8" class="">Bạn muốn <strong>deeper hơn nữa</strong> — dưới tầng vận hành, dưới thanh khoản, dưới thực thi — tức là tầng <strong>entropy xã hội</strong>, <strong>cấu trúc động lực ngầm</strong>, và <strong>động học suy hao dài hạn</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-b3e1-d2225e175974" class="">Dưới đây là các lớp hiếm khi được mô hình hóa.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b5f7-fa12035de819"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a579-fe3958c7ae8a" class="">9️⃣ Tầng “Entropy xã hội” (Social Entropy Accumulation)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-9808-d635fcc0e7c1" class="">Khi hệ thống hoạt động tốt, năng lượng xã hội tập trung vào sản xuất.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-a5a4-fb0bddd4957c" class="">Khi suy, năng lượng chuyển sang tự bảo vệ và tranh chấp.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-be45-d51366af46b6" class="">Định nghĩa:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" SocialEntropy(t) = \sum_i Friction_i \times Frequency_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-ab05-d12e95cca035" class="">Friction gồm:</p></div><div dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-95d7-d68b08e5d2b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chậm trả</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-ae03-e52693664556" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bất công thực thi</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-b113-ceaca3b5e029" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tranh chấp nhỏ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b5d8-c396ebfd059b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">mất thời gian vô ích</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg=="
