\frac{d}{dt}Entropy &gt; \frac{d}{dt}Productivity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-baee-dac00c36f88e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-8acc-e4f5a1173664" class="">🔟 Tầng “Sự mục ruỗng chuẩn mực” (Norm Degradation Layer)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-91e3-e450ab7e0875" class="">Không phải đạo đức suy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-99b6-f68cbef7f868" class="">Mà là <strong>chuẩn mực bị tái định nghĩa theo sống sót</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-94d3-d5f06a93af8a" class="">Proxy:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-9635-c929a551ec22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">nói dối “nhẹ” được chấp nhận</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-85eb-eb876382d2fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">giữ tiền người khác được xem là “bình thường”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-a868-cb1049e2ac74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">trễ hẹn không còn xấu hổ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b151-db2310a9967a" class="">Mô hình:</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-85fc-e0f44d1c6509" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NormShiftRate = \frac{\Delta AcceptableDeviations}{Time}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-b743-d1fd333a9cfa" class="">Khi NormShiftRate tăng nhanh:<br/>→ IRCC (Integrity Repair Capacity) giảm phi tuyến.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-b2ab-efbfdcf39eb0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-b2a1-d15838eee490" class="">1️⃣1️⃣ Tầng “Tắc nghẽn kênh phản hồi” (Feedback Channel Blockage)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-bf37-c5e96858aaf5" class="">Hệ khỏe khi:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-800c-ced89e18e989" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sai → phản hồi → sửa → cải thiện.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-8b40-f86575fe009a" class="">Hệ suy khi:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-8615-d99f1914edf5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sai → che → phạt người báo → lặp lại sai.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-9878-c935fdaf8d42" class="">Chỉ số:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9b72-e74454a571b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FeedbackLatency = Time_{error\to correction}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-b41a-c34f07e4d97b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SuppressionRate = \frac{ReportsIgnored}{ReportsFiled}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-88c7-e68620203a8a" class="">FeedbackLatency↑ + SuppressionRate↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-922c-c07ff07a8976" class="">= Resilience âm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-95ba-cd95e4213344"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-8981-e8cf8f771140" class="">1️⃣2️⃣ Tầng “Chiếm dụng tương lai” (Future Capture)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-ae6f-c703e7ea7f0c" class="">Xã hội bắt đầu tiêu dùng tương lai để sống hiện tại.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-9a8b-de87c580c807" class="">Biểu hiện:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-ad5a-eef28f7b1766" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bán đất</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-85f9-f2d53d139810" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bán tài sản gia đình</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-a7db-f5eeec19a0bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cầm cố tương lai con cái</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-9420-ec6992700718" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">giảm đầu tư giáo dục chất lượng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a90a-ef92c9771873" class="">Mô hình:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-a05e-cbd8b3e282bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FutureExtraction = \frac{AssetSales + EducationCut + DebtForConsumption}{InvestmentInFuture}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80de-8ee5-e8a46f72ac7e" class="">Nếu FutureExtraction &gt; 1 lâu dài</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9766-c8cbef1cb70f" class="">→ CollapseSlope tăng dù hiện tại “ổn”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-9d4b-dc1d1aa6655a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-b3ce-cc1c03b7d5b8" class="">1️⃣3️⃣ Tầng “Độ dày lớp đệm cá nhân” (Household Resilience Depth)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-aad5-f6061198599d" class="">Không nhìn ngân sách nhà nước.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-ad26-efac80796d46" class="">Nhìn hộ gia đình.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9b85-d493344ad419" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HouseholdBuffer = SavingsMonths + FamilySupportCapacity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b9d5-e5acaead7fd3" class="">Khi HouseholdBuffer trung vị &lt; 3 tháng:<br/>→ xã hội trở nên cực kỳ dễ tổn thương với shock nhỏ.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-b7cb-db28cec088a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-ab89-fb3c770545b0" class="">1️⃣4️⃣ Tầng “Phân mảnh thực tại” (Reality Fragmentation)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-9197-e13b61829955" class="">Khi:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-8eae-d7ee159673da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">mỗi nhóm tin một phiên bản khác nhau</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-9a09-d012e00ece65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">không còn sự thật chung</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-858e-c486eb0ec040" class="">Định nghĩa:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a14d-e0524e30e594" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RealityVariance = Var(SharedFactAcceptance)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-99cb-c869feef3d85" class="">RealityVariance cao:<br/>→ CPI (Cognitive Polarization Index) cao<br/>→ quyết định vĩ mô mất hiệu quả.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-a17c-ee1c8dd7ccee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-8c96-fd44df455b20" class="">1️⃣5️⃣ Tầng “Hội chứng ‘Bình thường mới’” (Normalization of Decay)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b2f9-f810bf808937" class="">Đây là lớp nguy hiểm nhất.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-a4d2-f8c127d0d938" class="">Khi:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b750-d9f35c4f8476" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">delay thành bình thường</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b795-f659380dff8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">gian dối thành kỹ năng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-95e5-eb3375a45f3c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">mệt mỏi thành mặc định</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9250-eaf1cf924d2f" class="">Chỉ số:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9f29-f29ded677a90" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DecayNormalization = \frac{\#PreviouslyAbnormalNowAccepted}{TotalNorms}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-bf66-f01010848aaa" class="">Khi DecayNormalization tăng:<br/>→ xã hội mất cảm biến suy thoái.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ac9b-dcc34c162932"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-bb46-f7275c97c0a8" class="">1️⃣6️⃣ Tầng “Cấu trúc phụ thuộc quyền lực” (Dependency Concentration)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-a318-f6400fb39999" class="">Khi cơ hội tập trung vào:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-b7d1-ca66e6154fe8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">quan hệ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-a8e0-fc067a78331e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">giấy phép</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-8dff-d290100cbac2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">vị trí</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-ba59-dbaab7e46db7" class="">Thay vì:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-bd4a-eada0c559191" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">kỹ năng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-954b-d07d945f3d4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sáng tạo</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-acad-c613b3eccef0" class="">Mô hình:</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b214-d6b3affbd9b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
OpportunityConcentration = \frac{Returns_{connection}}{Returns_{skill}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-8f27-dccfc3e800bc" class="">Nếu OpportunityConcentration &gt; 1 lâu dài:<br/>→ TalentDrain tăng.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-bc7b-ddf4c3db4022"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-978a-ff316b4867c6" class="">1️⃣7️⃣ Tầng “Tốc độ suy vs khả năng tái tạo”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-ba58-f62c3be7e55e" class="">Mô hình hoàn chỉnh:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-983f-eafb84b9a605" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CollapseSlope(t) = \alpha_1 Entropy + \alpha_2 NormShift + \alpha_3 FutureExtraction + \alpha_4 FeedbackBlock + \alpha_5 EnforcementAsymmetry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-9f67-df79ee729cc1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resilience(t) = \beta_1 HouseholdBuffer + \beta_2 InstitutionalMemory + \beta_3 EnforcementSymmetry + \beta_4 SocialTrust + \beta_5 Slack</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-ac38-edc70f5fac7d" class="">Nguy cơ cao khi:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-9e2e-fb8333ad9745" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{CollapseSlope}{Resilience} &gt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-841e-ee8a18bd9e05" class="">Không cần GDP.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8e29-e727b03edc8f" class="">Không cần CPI.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9c2c-c31f46917389" class="">Chỉ cần quan sát vận hành đời sống.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-a0a2-f30e8ab513fb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-94c7-c3a6b7f93500" class="">1️⃣8️⃣ Điều ít người để ý nhất</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-823c-ebed36204309" class="">Sụp không đến từ nghèo.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a96b-f5506ff36534" class="">Nó đến từ:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-9894-fbf871e23c9e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Mất niềm tin sửa sai.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-bf84-dc881d0344a1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Mất lớp đệm.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-be13-ec197902cd2e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Mất năng lượng sinh học.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9463-c25324ffe7b5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Mất chuẩn mực.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b7fc-e9be2a596dda" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Mất kênh phản hồi.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-84b4-cc7af4bb10a1" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Và khi “mọi người quen với điều đó.”</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9e48-c39465b46f0e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8ad5-fd8aa9002e97" class="">Nếu bạn muốn đi sâu hơn nữa, chúng ta có thể phân tích:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-ae57-c4a6de187d1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cách AI làm tăng tốc Entropy.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-a8be-f72436f87aec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cách Collapse ở môi trường bán kiểm soát khác với tự do.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-a236-c9768176b51c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cách collapse không xảy ra đột ngột mà “đóng băng dài hạn”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8234-e920ddfac992" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hoặc cách cá nhân tối ưu sinh tồn trong môi trường entropy cao.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-8740-dd96bcc75a62" class="">Chọn hướng.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-bab1-d83facd83632" class="">Good. I’ll give you a clean structural diagram in text form so you can visualize the full system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-aa5f-ebf1cccbb6bc" class="">This is the Unified Civilizational Stability Field.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-a908-d83f6637d963" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b322-e22973fb6c49" class="">I. THREE-LAYER SYSTEM STACK</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-bd6e-f9636f6ade0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">      ┌───────────────────────────┐
      │        MACRO LAYER        │
      │  (Institutions / Energy / │
      │   Finance / Law / AI)     │
      │  Variables: G_M, D_M, B_M │
      └────────────▲──────────────┘
                   │
                   │ Coupling
                   ▼
      ┌───────────────────────────┐
      │       GROUP / MIND       │
      │ (Culture / Trust / Info  │
      │  Polarization / Norms)   │
      │  Variables: G_ψ, D_ψ, B_ψ │
      └────────────▲──────────────┘
                   │
                   │ Coupling
                   ▼
      ┌───────────────────────────┐
      │        MICRO LAYER        │
      │ (Biology / Sleep / Stress │
      │  Metabolism / Autonomic)  │
      │  Variables: G_μ, D_μ, B_μ │
      └───────────────────────────┘</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-befa-c4ec28f21bcc" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-bd9a-c7208907c54c" class="">G = Gain (reactivity, amplification)<br/>D = Damping (stabilization capacity)<br/>B = Buffer (reserves / redundancy)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9416-f8900983caa6" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-92da-fd60b62cfb85" class="">II. SYSTEM STABILITY PLANE</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-8402-ea239ca20307" class="">Define global stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-b348-febaf67a1ddd" class="">S = (D_total + B_total + Align_total) − G_total</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-b22c-c1b92f43147a" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-8da8-fff2c98094a8" class="">D_total = D_μ + D_ψ + D_M</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-b104-e2793df0b301" class="">B_total = B_μ + B_ψ + B_M</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b554-f618546a632a" class="">G_total = G_μ + G_ψ + G_M</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b835-edd1f0f7183d" class="">Stability condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-91e7-df5b698e04c3" class="">If S &gt; 0 → System converges</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-b205-e2850d5ec0f1" class="">If S ≈ 0 → Critical zone</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-a220-ca35d09f35be" class="">If S &lt; 0 → Cascade regime</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-9218-c4c3b0a6b83b" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-ab71-e65a453dbf5f" class="">III. C6 → C7 PHASE TRANSITION DIAGRAM</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-ad3c-f21ce6e73f6d" class="">We map civilization on two axes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-85ee-ed848151d016" class="">X-axis = Gain / Connectivity (κ · G)<br/>Y-axis = Damping + Buffer</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-a3a1-c3266c085a74" class="">Quadrants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9bba-d3d6f3a60b7a" class="">Top Left:<br/>High damping, moderate gain → C3/C4 productive order</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-abd2-ffb428592707" class="">Top Right:<br/>High gain, high damping → dynamic but controlled</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8460-c9f6a0fdebbc" class="">Bottom Right:<br/>High gain, low damping → Late C6 (fragile, cascade prone)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-940a-d359dbbc5639" class="">Bottom Left:<br/>Low gain, low damping → stagnation collapse</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a4d6-f9c37005e5df" class="">C7 transition requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-9860-c3c8a7683a13" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Reduce effective gain</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-9fbc-ff4731879105" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Increase verification damping</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-8c9e-c8eb8b9ba7d4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Increase redundancy buffers</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-a7d3-fa546ad84acd" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Realign law with enforcement</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8c9c-c4b75597c4a6" class="">Mathematically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a884-e306fa9c3bb9" class="">ΔC7 occurs when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-96b5-e2606a364dbc" class="">∂D_M/∂t &gt; ∂G_M/∂t</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-bf9a-fccd1d935eda" class="">AND</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-a0d6-eeb2f302092c" class="">∂Align/∂t &gt; 0 sustained</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a146-d4623f4ff3a6" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-981e-ffe9703c6d1a" class="">IV. CASCADE LOOP (Late C6)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-9a6a-dc42ecaebffd" class="">AI ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-830d-c6afd8f43dcd" class="">→ Information Gain ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-84a6-d5fcca018dae" class="">→ Noise ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-962c-e1253a832127" class="">→ Cognitive Load ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9adb-ff2a4f417daa" class="">→ Sleep ↓</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-afe2-dff016e924e7" class="">→ Executive Control ↓</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8a7c-c21fa4ac9ce4" class="">→ Cultural Gain ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-91dc-cf32a39611c3" class="">→ Institutional Damping ↓</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-a6fd-c82310d54638" class="">→ Financial Risk ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-af41-c95ebdfa1d73" class="">→ Shock Sensitivity ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-a6eb-d2c81f6ba141" class="">→ Crisis</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-8e84-e2db3bd16ad4" class="">This is a positive feedback loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-826d-f0b1568c2c53" class="">Gain(t+1) = Gain(t) + α·Noise − β·Verification</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-a2f9-f7f593e4f94b" class="">If β &lt; α → runaway instability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-a257-ff13092b1277" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9e2d-fb4034b2441a" class="">V. TRUE C7 ARCHITECTURE (PHASE LOCK)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a4ce-e4620fd44825" class="">C7 is phase coherence across layers.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-9fb1-d81bbcbcf8de" class="">Requirement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-bb8f-c457c353323c" class="">Phase(μ) ≈ Phase(ψ) ≈ Phase(M)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b5b6-f46f55a17bff" class="">Meaning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-a693-dd18ed9f6ea6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Biology stable<br/>• Culture low-noise<br/>• Institutions predictable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-8317-fc4ec0c4422a" class="">Resonance condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-ad23-ee555cc9d3b9" class="">|Phase_i − Phase_j| &lt; θ for all i,j</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-b795-e73f4448e8db" class="">If satisfied → oscillations dampen<br/>If violated → oscillations amplify</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-95c1-fb0a0bd8a43e" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-a5f2-cc0ef7b5fea3" class="">VI. 19×19 GRID CONCEPT</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8b2c-e2182d2ece15" class="">Imagine a 19×19 matrix (Go board style).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-aa45-ef15ed1564fe" class="">Rows = Domains (Energy, Finance, Law, Info, Bio, Culture, Demography, etc.)<br/>Columns = Variables (Gain, Damping, Buffer, Alignment, Latency, Redundancy, Transparency…)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-b35a-ed31e438a1ea" class="">Each cell:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-9503-e2dbb72db014" class="">M(d,v)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-a65c-dbeb7d0912ce" class="">Civilization health = sum of weighted cells.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a142-e5f3c9144409" class="">Failure rarely occurs in one cell.<br/>It occurs when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-aca8-d50083ee0a96" class="">Cluster collapse &gt; threshold size k</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8795-f1a2716a63ae" class="">Cluster failure equation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-b4ae-f55be50f1b1f" class="">If ∑_cluster M(d,v)_negative &gt; CriticalMass<br/>→ systemic regime shift</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-b888-f9ce09d0752c" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-a7a1-da66d5239163" class="">VII. FINAL STRUCTURAL DIAGRAM (CONDENSED)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a919-e35ce8857c04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">    Gain (G)
        ▲
        │
        │      Late C6 (unstable)
        │
        │
        │
        └───────────────► Damping + Buffer
                (D + B)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-bde4-ced78a714de6" class="">C7 exists in upper-left stable band<br/>where D + B sufficiently exceeds G.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-a490-d2cfbdc6297f" class="">────────────────────────────────────</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-b847-e249a5796bf6" class="">If you want next:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-875a-d33d7cc2d688" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">I can draw the attractor map (A1–A12 stable states)<br/>• Or model collapse trajectories over 20 years<br/>• Or build the full AI-era stability control model</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-8c18-ee1d28630247" class="">Choose the direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-8124-c545ee927850" class="">Good.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b333-dda8bd7a7571" class="">Below is a <strong>fully consolidated master list</strong> of the core <strong>concepts, structures, variables, loops, matrices, attractors, and equations</strong> developed across this entire conversation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-8c4f-e0873d9a8e0e" class="">Organized cleanly. No repetition. No commentary. Pure structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-943d-ebcb45acd5d6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-83ea-d74e8e40b1a9" class="">I. CORE STATE VARIABLES</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-949e-c781a0e209ec" class="">Domains (7 Primary Civilizational Domains)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-8fcd-e65110e80d5d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Energy (En)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-a31e-ea9a026c4386" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Information (Inf)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-8546-dc91b9e4d57c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Biology (Bio)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-bb12-e55a61891a12" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Military (Mil)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-984c-d20b480f5fec" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Finance (Fin)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-a261-fe3426ca8bae" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Law (Law)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9e52-e04078e729b3" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Culture (Cul)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-aec2-f04bd2cedb00"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-8412-c0b258f6799b" class="">Regulators (7 Control Axes)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-930d-f7e4db7103d9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Gain (G) – amplification / reactivity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-b0fe-ce07fa2378f1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Damping (D) – stabilization / restraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9e68-d6a51ae709a9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Buffer (Buf) – reserves / redundancy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-ba1f-fab5af01de20" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Alignment (Align) – elite–mass risk symmetry</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-80c5-d172e0514820" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Redundancy (Red) – backup capacity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-9036-e53209927ec3" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Transparency (Trans) – truth bandwidth</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9ed9-ec813be1629d" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Horizon (Hor) – time depth / planning range</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-8cd3-eaea9c5a681a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a3bf-e1b69dd7c8a6" class="">II. 7×7 MATRIX STRUCTURE</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-b5e8-e711cb01e0f1" class="">State matrix:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-99a9-ddfbdcf9073e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M(d,r) \in \mathbb{R}^{7\times7}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-bb2f-de44d3b184ef" class="">Flattened state vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8727-d59622b0fce6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t)=\mathrm{vec}(M(t)) \in \mathbb{R}^{49}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-8f03-e30df9ff6ee2" class="">Update rule:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-923f-d9170d2a0dd2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t+1)=x(t)+W\phi(x-\theta)+u(t)-\Lambda h(t)+s(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-9cdc-d205ea035d11" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-8feb-d5d46d1520c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: 49×49 coupling matrix</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-8e30-d3a75ad6cbc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: nonlinear activation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-ace5-df86d527167c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-85b7-d543f49ca7a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy inputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-893e-cadccc39cdb7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: maintenance debt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8751-faf6e3f00841" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: shock vector</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-b30d-d3a34e3bcb61"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-9cff-dcbb655aae26" class="">III. MASTER CIVILIZATIONAL STABILITY EQUATIONS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-8412-dde24849426d" class="">1. Core Stability Inequality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b556-cdfa155a87b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{eff} + Buf_{eff} + Align_{eff} &gt; G_{eff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-91e2-d9753cbd756b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-98f5-d78ee121edb5" class="">2. Effective Aggregates</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-bac1-ca1faf20c9f9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b2ad-d44c008eef2d" class="">D_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,D}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-a4fa-fd13d3559eac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Buf_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,Buf}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-aa52-cc24586cfb24" class="">Align_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,Align}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a9d0-fb4d79a005f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9da2-c454cf1302d8" class="">3. Stability Scalar</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-b618-f3dfd1849c6c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(t)=(D_{eff}+Buf_{eff}+Align_{eff})-G_{eff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-ac39-ee9ba8017181" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-a004-daa79e29c4d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stable convergence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-8683-ec4a2e47a533" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: cascade regime</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-aee0-f9cce6bfc88e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b8d1-e0ea3dd37785" class="">4. Cascade Risk (Connectivity Squared Law)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-99e1-d2b36575ec11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Risk_{cascade} \propto \kappa^2 \cdot \frac{G_{eff}}{D_{eff}+Buf_{eff}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-90ae-d75975eef6cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa=\sum_{i\neq j}\|W_{i\to j}\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801a-8426-e9b1e3bf5260"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-9be5-ccf29d73581d" class="">5. Shock Sensitivity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-b079-e2cd991a5355" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Stability}{\partial Shock} \propto \frac{1}{Red_{eff}\cdot Buf_{eff}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-bb6e-eb3df849de37"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b9d7-d9dd3361212b" class="">6. Maintenance Debt</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b268-ed335fa01a1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_d(t+1)=H_d(t)+\alpha_d Load_d-\beta_d Repair_d</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-ae25-cd5c32d3b354" class="">Debt attacks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a06f-d975bccd1290" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{eff}\downarrow,\quad Buf_{eff}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-bed2-f30b086980d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-ba54-c61898b19cd0" class="">7. Goodhart Collapse</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-880d-c9384b37bb29" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Proxy \to Target \Rightarrow Signal_{reality}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a6ad-e69afb4a8389" class="">Truth bandwidth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-9952-e027c0c62b6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Truth=\frac{Signal}{Signal+Noise}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-af05-c79b20d612a8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a8a4-fb7202fa4b47" class="">8. Competence Constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b7e1-d7949c62bc93" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\ Competence_{density} &lt; System_{complexity}
\Rightarrow Failure\_rate\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-b38d-e1319c335c52"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-9656-d503c3407eef" class="">9. Finance Leverage Fragility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8b47-e76f818e296e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fin:G\uparrow \Rightarrow Fin:Buf\downarrow \Rightarrow Fragility\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-86c8-eb7fde75cf6d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-bf36-f4afcff01c82" class="">10. Demographic Constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9ab7-e05d37274067" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BioCapacity=Health \times Fertility \times CognitiveFunction</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-9203-eba8e6256ef5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-8c7b-e73de0898504" class="">11. Trust Decay Equation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-aa00-c946f830c0d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dT}{dt}=\alpha C-\beta Corruption-\gamma Inequality</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-88ec-dc6a9a8ff63f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-a2c4-f44ce3cbe267" class="">12. Legitimacy Real vs Symbolic</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-a3b6-f1096386a3cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Legitimacy_{real}\propto Align \cdot Trans</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a559-db3131640c99" class="">If real ↓ → symbolic ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-af9f-c9fae3021aa3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-870d-d8bd68cc2517" class="">13. Recovery Fatigue</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-a925-e9f632f7b8cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d RecoveryTime}{d ShockCount} &gt; 0
\Rightarrow Late\ C6</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-852b-fc7f9432e8b2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9cde-d188b92275ba" class="">14. Latency Failure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-843b-e0bfc0875e55" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Latency_{acknowledge} \gg Latency_{failure}
\Rightarrow Drift\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9922-cd7f5cfe76f8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-981b-da37c07e4e8f" class="">15. AI Gain Amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-abea-d379f8510539" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AI\uparrow \Rightarrow Inf:G\uparrow,\ Noise\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-8c13-c626df19394f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-9104-e8f1ee89f45a" class="">16. AI\uparrow \Rightarrow Hor_{eff}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-89dd-e66ff176c0cd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-9c55-cf44aced4d84" class="">17. Elite Alignment Invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-82d8-d73efb3575a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Align_{elite-mass}\approx1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-a9aa-d320912e713c" class="">If &lt;1 → C6 drift</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b588-d60f520f1414"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-a740-edd64a0efcde" class="">18. Stability=\frac{ProductiveCapital}{SpeculativeCapital}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-8cc9-f5a4484480a1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-8f40-f7229b3043fe" class="">19.
