Buffer &lt; Exposure \Rightarrow Shock\ Inevitability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b355-e068b25fa7fe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a9a4-c0cf7c8ded98" class="">20. Gain-Damping Meta-Law</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-836a-dec586013294" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gain &gt; Damping \Rightarrow Oscillation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-a2fe-cdbfc87c224d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-926d-ed7bccec9339" class="">IV. MICRO ↔ MACRO UNIFIED FIELD MODEL</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b450-e4ad90f36d1b" class="">State vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-be1d-cae5ad81f955" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{bmatrix}
x_\mu\\x_\psi\\x_M
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-961f-de6cc99e1801" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8260-d1e7b50e80b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: biological layer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a213-f304b7eb5b03" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: psychological/group layer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-bcb5-c6500877e92e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: institutional layer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-9b06-f5a38d820651" class="">Coupled dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9298-ca3470dfaa3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=x_t+F(x)+s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-b44e-d78f3674dd64"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-b41c-c9358baa1401" class="">Bottom-Up Loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b5dd-e27f9909b3f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Sleep\downarrow \Rightarrow ExecFunction\downarrow
\Rightarrow Impulse\uparrow \Rightarrow Cul:G\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-b5ec-f21c29c590ac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-87da-e0dd1c976edb" class="">Top-Down Loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-ae82-cf44105ed4d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Law:Align\downarrow \Rightarrow Stress\uparrow
\Rightarrow Inflammation\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8ae1-ee8923235228"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a1e8-e8fc2360f2b3" class="">Resonance Condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-9fd8-c3f04e0da934" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|Phase_i-Phase_j|&lt;\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9915-ec48b023f368" class="">C7 requires phase lock across μ, ψ, M.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-ad76-c7c63bca35f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-925a-cd32f845fcc6" class="">Total Damping</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-a70a-df04c9eacb32" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{total}=D_\mu+D_\psi+D_M</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8a61-fbcd6df09c2c" class="">False stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-b547-d80014478f43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_M\uparrow,\ D_\mu\downarrow,\ D_\psi\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-87ff-e985095a6179"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-ad9e-c5cc6a3621d1" class="">V. ATTRACTORS (A1–A12)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-8be6-f3e16c0c62bc" class="">A1: High-Gain Oscillation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-8298-e327574a3975" class="">A2: Speculation Trap</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-806b-e70532b675db" class="">A3: Selective Enforcement Basin</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-9293-e24e887082a6" class="">A4: False C7 Compression</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-80c5-c2768b407175" class="">A5: C6→C7 Pivot Window</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8591-e42b560d11ee" class="">A6: Technocratic Rebuild</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-b6a3-e45d7b5eac2f" class="">A7: Trust Reconstitution</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-817e-c596a20d6b7a" class="">A8: Buffer-first Stabilization</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-9ef2-ee33bbf94549" class="">A9: C7 Islands in C6 Sea</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-8b76-e2dc63f7f168" class="">A10: Innovation Escape</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-980b-e65edb0c25df" class="">A11: Demographic Drag Basin</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a37d-d28aaa88c76a" class="">A12: C8 Self-Damped Equilibrium</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-bff4-f2e9e10ee43a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-8182-d92efbc01e15" class="">VI. STAGE DEFINITIONS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a156-eff3012db31b" class="">C5: High coherence, repair ≥ decay</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a607-d5411f69c4e4" class="">C6: Gain-dominant fragmentation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-99cd-fd6c997f7be6" class="">C7: Damped rebuild</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-a83f-eff1e6981eaa" class="">C8: Self-stabilizing equilibrium</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-bf5a-d13d9c2ad75b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8f06-d09d2d36cb4f" class="">VII. CLUSTER FAILURE CONDITION</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-839a-cdbd0e9841cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{cluster} NegativeCells &gt; CriticalMass
\Rightarrow RegimeShift</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8bcb-e0644613d9cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-8e34-eedce71a543e" class="">VIII. 19×19 GRID EXTENSION</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-9ab6-ee7df2355bc6" class="">Rows = Domains</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-8b53-fd54dfe2cb03" class="">Columns = Variables</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-aad4-f9a2a0e4723f" class="">Failure occurs when contiguous degradation cluster size ≥ k.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-a183-dc0fce326e07"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-83a2-d763441364ab" class="">IX. MASTER META-INVARIANTS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-a54d-f5a477fb0431" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Complexity &gt; Repair Capacity → Fragility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-b598-fa0a56a9d448" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Abstraction &gt; Ground Truth → Distortion</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-8baa-ebc28c4dc144" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Noise &gt; Signal → Trust Collapse</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-87b2-f4a06f7c2de1" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Extraction &gt; Alignment → Legitimacy Decay</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ae37-e7a804431a4d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Comfort &gt; Adaptation Pressure → Competence Drift</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-a56e-de115df4378d" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Connectivity² &gt; Damping → Cascade</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-8e5a-fd553f3d9367" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Elite Exit &gt; Domestic Commitment → Internal Collapse</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-95c5-fc5e79d533f6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-92df-da8dd1f013f6" class="">X. C7 ENTRY CONDITIONS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-8249-eb507c82ca83" class="">Simultaneous increase in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-ac22-e8fd9811f6c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta Trans_{Law}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-8ad7-e8de3dc1d811" class="">\Delta Hor_{Fin}&gt;0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-bb78-da924c609100" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta Red_{En}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-ab79-e5ed8df2fd55" class="">Sustained over cycles.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-83d6-f2b3c456766f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-990c-d93383e64fb3" class="">This is the full structural system assembled in this conversation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8816-c5f8ca8eab46" class="">If you want next, we can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-b5de-f788438af9ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Collapse everything into a single unified civilizational field equation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9f74-c188b8876781" class="">• Or simulate a hypothetical country through C6→C7</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-92c0-de6098dfbd58" class="">• Or compress into 10 universal invariants only</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-aac6-e3b8180ca405" class="">Choose.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-82cb-dbf11676348c" class="">XXIII. Deeper: executable semantics layer (contradiction grammar, complexity metric, MECE validator, threshold calculus, rewrite write-sets)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9758-f620f850cc6e" class="">This layer specifies the remaining undefined functions so the system can be executed deterministically.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-9bfa-c07622e37b67"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-8186-ed1901b1f4bb" class="">1) Contradiction detection as a formal grammar</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-ab57-cfbc9dd2c8da" class="">Every claim  is normalized into a canonical 6-tuple:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b4d0-f5221b69f448" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{NF}(c)=(s,\;p,\;o,\;q,\;t,\;\kappa)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-aac1-d4f4e919f631" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: subject identifier (entity set)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a34a-ff4c7d90ad62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: predicate identifier (relation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-a5a3-f653f033e6c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: object identifier (value / entity set)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-8ae1-d1d09d4e6548" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: quantifier (ALL, EXISTS, MOST, SOME, NONE)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-8ebc-f79438135235" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: time window</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9567-ea6b3a728393" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: context constraints (set of conditions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9577-e391f9c8a840" class="">1.1 Overlap predicates</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-b7f8-d53c849784aa" class="">Subject overlap:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-a6ba-d5e77c6e5264" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Ov}_S(c_i,c_j)=1 \iff s_i\cap s_j\neq\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-9a6d-fd30a13b9ae1" class="">Time overlap:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-aade-f26529b7bdb3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Ov}_T(c_i,c_j)=1 \iff [t_i]\cap[t_j]\neq\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-962a-c3c78b01e7d7" class="">Context compatibility:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b42e-e458da89156e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Compat}_\kappa(c_i,c_j)=1 \iff \kappa_i\cup\kappa_j\ \text{is satisfiable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-a344-c5750e631559" class="">1.2 Predicate polarity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-92cf-cc2769e428c8" class="">Each predicate  has a polarity operator  (its explicit negation). Example: “increases” vs “decreases”, “allowed” vs “not allowed”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-9589-dca736feb1c7" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-943e-ec0eb1ed038c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{NegPair}(p_i,p_j)=1 \iff p_j=\neg p_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-aa75-e0dd29cfc42f" class="">1.3 Contradiction rule set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a40f-f680f5fe59fb" class="">Two claims contradict iff they speak about overlapping subject/time/context and assert negated predicates about the same object (or mutually exclusive objects under same predicate).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-be05-f9516ef91839" class="">Primary contradiction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-a2d1-e0f11da98b5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)=1 \iff
\mathrm{Ov}_S=1\wedge \mathrm{Ov}_T=1\wedge \mathrm{Compat}_\kappa=1\wedge ( \mathrm{NegPair}(p_i,p_j)=1)\wedge (o_i=o_j)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a48d-d0d8600ac569" class="">Mutual-exclusion contradiction (values cannot co-hold):<br/>Let  be a domain table (finite) defining mutually exclusive values under predicate .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-aa5d-dca9b6d20d32" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)=1 \iff
\mathrm{Ov}_S\wedge \mathrm{Ov}_T\wedge \mathrm{Compat}_\kappa\wedge (p_i=p_j)\wedge \mathrm{Mutex}(o_i,o_j,p_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-be3d-f473f0444cf8" class="">Quantifier contradiction (ALL vs EXISTS-NOT within same overlap):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-9766-c1cac45d9ada" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)=1 \iff
\mathrm{Ov}_S\wedge \mathrm{Ov}_T\wedge \mathrm{Compat}_\kappa\wedge (p_i=p_j)\wedge (o_i=o_j)\wedge
\Big[(q_i=\mathrm{ALL}\wedge q_j=\mathrm{EXISTS\_NOT})\ \lor\ (q_j=\mathrm{ALL}\wedge q_i=\mathrm{EXISTS\_NOT})\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a661-c5b4a33363eb" class="">This makes contradiction detection finite and computable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-80ac-c34c6f1cd35e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b61f-d09b3bb01302" class="">2) Complexity measure and epistemic budget</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-a956-f73adef82716" class="">Interpretation  is represented as a finite set of asserted propositions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-b8f4-eb2e0b63d66d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{E}=\{e_1,\dots,e_m\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-ac78-df948f895cec" class="">2.1 Description-length complexity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-82a9-f8a2fa11cec1" class="">Assign each proposition a normalized token length  (or AST node count). Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-8f1d-ddc8e2e1a2e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K(\hat{E})=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\ell(e_i)\;+\;\lambda \cdot |\mathrm{Deps}(\hat{E})|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-859e-db5c8655178d" class="">2.2 Budget dynamics</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80de-80b8-dbb67e109020" class="">Budget increases only through measured acquisition events  (new evidence objects):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-89c7-e62d56b5dbfc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_{t+1}=b_t + \alpha \cdot |\Delta E^{meas}_t| - \beta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9846-c51e91e33aed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: decay (forgetting / staleness)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8236-c2c204a91c0c" class="">2.3 Budget gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a79b-e46c575eee0c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{501}=1 \iff K(\hat{E}_t)\le b_t\ \lor\ \tau(\hat{E}_t)=\mathrm{MB}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9bbb-f086c800dd5e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{502}=1 \iff \tau(\hat{E}_{t-1})=\mathrm{MB}\ \wedge\ \tau(\hat{E}_t)\neq\mathrm{MB}\ \Rightarrow\ |\Delta E^{meas}_t|&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a148-ee72f36c4b3f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8f86-dafb7c555194" class="">3) MECE validator for Rule-of-2 and Rule-of-4 (executable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-9d11-d80e20ee377e" class="">Let a decomposition be a set family  over universe  (the parent construct).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9395-f0ac674fd7c8" class="">3.1 Coverage</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-9d61-f60d80813cc1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Cover}(\mathcal{D},U)=1 \iff \bigcup_{i=1}^{k} D_i = U</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b10a-fbe30f033461" class="">3.2 Exclusivity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-84d8-dfe4b6120951" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Excl}(\mathcal{D})=1 \iff \forall i\neq j:\ D_i\cap D_j=\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-927a-f2e392b1d89d" class="">3.3 Non-empty bins</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-8a56-cb8d98f648cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{NonEmpty}(\mathcal{D})=1 \iff \forall i:\ |D_i|&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b50a-c5c63527b076" class="">3.4 Rule validators</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-b686-d8dc244d57b4" class="">Rule-of-2 (exactly ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-8aec-fe31b245968d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{R2OK}(U,D_1,D_2)=\mathrm{Cover}\wedge \mathrm{Excl}\wedge \mathrm{NonEmpty}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-8ce2-cee23ecbfd5b" class="">Rule-of-4 (exactly ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-99ec-c6b98c09f5ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{R4OK}(U,\{D_{11},D_{12},D_{21},D_{22}\})=\mathrm{Cover}\wedge \mathrm{Excl}\wedge \mathrm{NonEmpty}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-b9cc-f9b98eb824ab" class="">If  is not enumerable (conceptual universe), enforce an explicit <strong>enumeration protocol</strong>:  must be expressed as a finite list of atomic items (claims, requirements, invariants, metrics, steps). Otherwise the decomposition is invalid by measurement integrity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9a1b-c821994f4140"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-ae19-d8f67ec748c5" class="">4) Threshold calculus (how are defined)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a968-d174c752d6da" class="">No free thresholds. Every threshold must be derived from a windowed baseline.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-a563-ec67a72993b1" class="">4.1 Baseline estimation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-9128-c62b2ce07e86" class="">For any scalar observable , define a rolling baseline over window :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-a957-cceadf32f8c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mu_W(t)=\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=0}^{W-1} y_{t-i}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-9bdf-dae987935749" class="">\sigma_W(t)=\sqrt{\frac{1}{W-1}\sum_{i=0}^{W-1} (y_{t-i}-\mu_W(t))^2}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-a1e4-fee33a27f98e" class="">4.2 Threshold definition (policy-controlled)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-922c-dfaa9d9c397d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta_y(t)=\mu_W(t) - \gamma \sigma_W(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-8494-f0fd768110ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta_y(t)=\mu_W(t) + \gamma \sigma_W(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8412-d8ea6a6912c2" class="">4.3 Drift metrics</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-8d51-e375096988b5" class="">Internal drift on biological vector :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-aaab-c6490244e5e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{\text{Internal}}(t)=\| \mathrm{Norm}(B_t)-\mathrm{Norm}(B_{t-1}) \|_1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8ce3-c8d525cd16d7" class="">Feedback drift:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b71d-f6bff730fe04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{\text{Feedback}}(t)=d(F_t,F_{t-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-bde5-caa8962812e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">edit distance if feedback is text</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-becf-d08267362584" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">absolute difference if numeric</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-94e5-c1ef663605b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hamming distance if categorical</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-942e-eed994ceb5ea" class="">Set tolerances as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9748-d57220d9e066" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\epsilon_I=\eta_I\cdot \mathbb{E}[\Delta_{\text{Internal}}]_{W}
\qquad
\epsilon_F=\eta_F\cdot \mathbb{E}[\Delta_{\text{Feedback}}]_{W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-98d7-c41724215ff1" class="">This makes thresholds Only formal architecture, constraints, and findings.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-a9fb-c419e73b093a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-bcd0-de3628078d12" class="">I. SYSTEM PURPOSE</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-8209-f55756e24e7b" class="">A deterministic, closed-loop integrity architecture that unifies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-a2fa-f8fa210b4eb1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Biological regulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-b82a-eaca622a7b84" class="">• Cognitive governance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-bb49-c58bc64897c1" class="">• Artifact auditing</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-9c61-c4135be5753c" class="">• Measurement discipline</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9a2a-e0e07d0781f8" class="">• Ownership control</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9690-f3f75d1b89df" class="">• Epistemic boundedness</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-9901-f69b1ff7700f" class="">• Rewrite determinism</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-8f3d-de79f958a8d4" class="">• Drift stabilization</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-aff6-e1aa90e39c76" class="">• Release sealing</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-98d6-e1df0c097c53" class="">Everything reduces to a single rule:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a115-edf59c71c812" class="">No state transition without invariant satisfaction or deterministic rewrite.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-aa6f-eb6588d308b3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-9d35-f878b882db3f" class="">II. TOTAL SYSTEM STRUCTURE</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-aacc-ee48f00ff9b4" class="">1. State Space</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-b828-c46921dec3c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\mathcal{X}
=
\mathcal{B}
\times
\mathcal{G}
\times
\mathcal{K}
\times
\mathcal{E}
\times
\mathcal{O}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-9911-f8109b9613db" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-aa64-e65a1248f2d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: Biological / behavioral state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-953e-d3d1797c9d3e" class="">• : Governance / policy / invariants</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9544-ed56b276aec2" class="">• : Knowledge / artifact / claim graph</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-bc08-cef456ef8db2" class="">• : Environment / feedback</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-89e6-d3ca22d2b639" class="">• : Ownership constraints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-b715-e5ee9ac25bd8" class="">All components are measurable or explicitly tagged ModelBounded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-950e-e6200fa5ac22" class="">No hidden state permitted.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8469-e67c4fbf7cac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b44e-ce771b2c46e1" class="">III. BIOLOGICAL LAYER</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-93a5-d4324780513f" class="">Observable biological vector</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-97f7-fe13d7abde78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t=(x_t,r_t,m_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bae3-e44115c2063e" class="">Physiology</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-935f-c81a950b43da" class="">HR, HRV, RR, BP, Temp, SleepScore, AccelVar, PostureStability, etc.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-b57e-cd72876643b2" class="">Regulation plan</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b98a-e386388e01b6" class="">SleepPlan, NutritionPlan, MovementPlan, BreathProtocol, WorkBlock.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-ae13-e5eef67bdbff" class="">Execution performance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-870b-c268305b00be" class="">ReactionTime, ErrorRate, TaskLatency, CompletionRate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-a5f1-c1ba35140f29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-853a-ea39dd59cd49" class="">UBI Domain Vector</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9266-fe7fbb768e0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{u}_t=
\begin{bmatrix}
u_{NB}\\
u_{NE}\\
u_S\\
u_{BE}
\end{bmatrix}
\in[0,1]^4</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8a19-ec3b7d128524" class="">Each domain computed from measurable features.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-a125-d97932a00dee" class="">Absolute Biological Integrity occurs when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-86eb-c9a008cc3c13" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_i u_i \ge 1-\delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-96ec-fb0d4a03b1f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-83de-d1e9f2de26b0" class="">IV. TELEMETRY FUSION</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b65a-f59cf5c58a72" class="">Telemetry sources:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9cb6-e34e730bc370" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t=

z^{exp}\\
z^{log}\\
z^{sys}\\
z^{sp}
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-bbab-dad6ac174ce4" class="">Spiritual telemetry allowed only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-9698-d42c8c6c3144" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c\in\Lambda_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-a699-f9d6871f53fd" class="">Otherwise zero-weighted.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-85e3-dc314649702c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-8023-e73fb78a14f8" class="">V. CLAIM GRAPH + UCIA</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-9dec-f987a70209ae" class="">Artifacts compile to graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-861d-ead54c7b93f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C=(V,A)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-b2d6-dcf0565e04c1" class="">Each claim:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a2b4-ca28dc6cee1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c=(s,p,o,q,t,\kappa,\tau,\omega)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-8481-f713b9f658d9" class="">Support types:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-bb29-ce2546c589e6" class="">Emp, Inf, Def, MB, Prim, Lim</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8b5e-d1af6f925217"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-9993-f9b90ba992e3" class="">Discharge rules</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b269-c76bc06f1bb7" class="">Claim admissible iff proof obligation discharged.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-ac8f-c50e87d56b85" class="">Graph invalid if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-8fca-df8b91e175e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradiction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-99e6-d611c247f1e5" class="">• illegal cycle</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-9b0a-ed730055767c" class="">• orphan</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-8500-cbb6983ae222" class="">• undischarged claim</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b955-fd82c244b450" class="">• unbounded universal</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-acc9-d20f79689f9e" class="">• terminal smuggling</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-b987-c251ec515fac" class="">• metric undefined</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-afad-e6c630d9933e" class="">• ownership unknown</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-bc31-fba85bc5da87" class="">• epistemic overbudget</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-b786-da5712d33367" class="">• drift exceeded</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-a671-d69673278aad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-9454-e7a1b1933fd7" class="">VI. CONTRADICTION GRAMMAR</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-8064-cd2f3f61a597" class="">Two claims contradict iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a2c2-d9bb32231b69" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">subject overlap</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-8395-f2e4bb2a90c2" class="">• time overlap</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-b0d2-f6827af559da" class="">• context compatible</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8999-cc6120c3704f" class="">• predicate negation OR mutex objects</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-8b47-cbabaf00c349" class="">• incompatible quantifiers</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-87f2-c5e53c68fc3f" class="">Fully formalized as boolean predicates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b9a4-d0219305dbaf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-88f0-d8c208af6658" class="">VII. INVARIANT LIBRARY</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-81f7-f52618a44bb0" class="">Grouped invariants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-a6a0-ed12131756bb" class="">A — Graph structure (I001–I003)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-967e-c09845546a70" class="">B — UCIA discharge (I101–I106)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-9403-e43d02b6d1cf" class="">C — Measurement integrity (I201–I204)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-b6b9-e20e6577469b" class="">D — R2/R4 completeness (I301–I303)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-b363-ede648a63dc2" class="">E — Ownership compliance (I401–I403)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8e95-df9b619f4306" class="">F — Epistemic budget (I501–I502)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-9549-c6e63d540f8c" class="">G — Drift and multi-rate separation (I601–I602)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-9b33-d65c7031eb8a" class="">Validity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8816-c68b72d26cee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{V}(X)=\prod_k I_k(X)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-af91-e734f7488731" class="">Minimality condition defined via witness states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-ab4d-dd46599cd3d7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8783-f84c14308863" class="">VIII. OWNERSHIP CALCULUS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b408-c803689afb41" class="">Ownership lattice:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-82c7-fab63f5a5c55" class="">Self &lt; Public &lt; Licensed &lt; Unknown &lt; Restricted</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-b003-f7f8d19f45a7" class="">Join operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-88dc-ed89c11a9cc7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\omega(c)=\bigsqcup \omega(\text{deps and evidence})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b7dc-db714a5432fe" class="">Export allowed only if ownership exportable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-bf91-d47505a2eea4" class="">Unknown → invariant failure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-84ef-cb815821fb94"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a5c4-d76af8f5e22b" class="">IX. K(\hat{E})=\sum \ell(e_i) + \lambda |\mathrm{Deps}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-bdab-c9eb512df418" class="">Budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-9b62-d28f57c08ee6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_{t+1}=b_t + \alpha |\Delta E^{meas}| - \beta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8cd2-e0d2695d851e" class="">Constraint:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b95b-dde38453d78c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K(\hat{E}) \le b_t \text{ OR MB-tagged}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-8cfa-d3b97e5b9605" class="">No forced promotion without evidence delta.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-ae0b-e208b8e08f88"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-934d-f5d419e7d52c" class="">X. \Delta_{Internal} = \|Norm(B_t)-Norm(B_{t-1})\|_1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-9e91-d552b7c173d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{Feedback}=d(F_t,F_{t-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-9ce2-d4d54410bc48" class="">Thresholds derived from rolling baseline.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-93a1-ecec069db88e" class="">Violation triggers safe projection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-895d-d725b895de6c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8e79-fdb529b3559b" class="">XI. SAFE PROJECTION</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8e95-ed0c2dac5349" class="">Safe region  absorbing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-8d2c-dc4223c8c526" class="">Projection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-87d3-f1f389544a53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{t+1}=\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(X_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8983-e2d92c4df789" class="">Actions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-9c0f-f95ff548e637" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">freeze artifact promotion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-9389-f78e2a109a33" class="">• restrict allowance set</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b8a2-eef9aefe57d0" class="">• enforce conservative regulation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-bd96-d4ffa5093604" class="">• append fail log</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-8ebf-c1464eb55a63"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-840a-c19fdce2dff7" class="">XII.
