VERSION SEALING</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-97f3-d4ae1a46975c" class="">Hash artifacts and policies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-ad82-d58258f2e766" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_D=\mathrm{SHA256}(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-8e4e-dd1cd193a9d4" class="">h_P=\mathrm{SHA256}(P)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b39c-d890c5bab8a8" class="">Sealed release if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-b24c-c3e01bd3e545" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Valid</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bf5b-dd807fc21aa4" class="">• no failcodes</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8369-e21a36c2952a" class="">• hashes stable over window</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9db0-c5ee8e498917"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-b357-e31ed615ac90" class="">XIV. STABILITY + ATTRACTOR</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-9ee8-dc705abf2b1f" class="">Define Lyapunov-like function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-84c6-ed2302f178fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L(X)=
w_1\cdot \#\mathrm{Violations}
+w_2\cdot \#\mathrm{Undischarged}
+w_3\cdot H(C)
+w_4\cdot \mathbb{1}[OwnershipFail]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-8a59-e25b03654874" class="">Convergence condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-81f8-d1251ab451a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L(X_{t+1})&lt;L(X_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-ab16-f2691d2d84bd" class="">Integrity attractor:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8d63-ee25e57e6c4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X^*=\mathcal{M}(X^*)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-a035-cb9cc645051a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-9869-f13ab5241d8c" class="">XV. COMPUTATIONAL COMPLEXITY</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8381-ee8c2838ae12" class="">Claim extraction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b2b1-cba1bc10fd63" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O(|D|)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8844-d25878cca6e5" class="">Graph + SCC:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-808a-fad589275fc4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O(n+m)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-ac96-c2de28a09b8e" class="">Invariant checks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-8739-c575da3a46eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O(K(n+m))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-ad5c-d9755853a745" class="">Total:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-91cf-ce466b2b8b8f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O(|D| + (K+1)(n+m))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-90e4-c3e0a79eb83c" class="">Linear in artifact size.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-81be-e1c6e7769cd9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b349-ed66f73f74e9" class="">XVI. MASTER TRANSITION</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-8020-f130139d9915" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{t+1}=
\begin{cases}
\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(X_t) &amp; \Gamma(X_t)=1 \\
\mathcal{R}^*(\mathrm{failcode})(X_t) &amp; \mathrm{failcode}\neq \varnothing \\
\mathcal{M}(X_t) &amp; \text{if Valid and sealed}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-8074-f01d86e44275" class="">No undefined transitions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-8f14-f52d7d68ac41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-95ba-ca08b7226a8c" class="">XVII. GUARANTEES ACHIEVED</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-9c44-fed570eb30ea" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>No contradiction can survive.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8072-c5c7d7aeb718" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>No unbounded universal claim allowed.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-ba66-e4fa0e94cf50" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>No metric without source/unit/rate.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e9-bcfc-f8e300bc2d05" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>No ownership ambiguity allowed.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-aeb7-c7b05c1fcf2e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>No promotion without measurable evidence.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-adea-dfb0bdf4b838" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>No fast biological oscillation rewriting policy.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-accc-e38d780a6a00" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>No rewrite without logging.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-bf17-e34c382cfd0f" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>No MB promotion without delta evidence.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-a602-d3b24e7639ac" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>No decomposition without MECE validation.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-b99d-cb356876d09c" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>No drift beyond tolerance without safe projection.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-b141-c6ab557352e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-ad5f-cc8cc88086c8" class="">XVIII. FINAL FINDINGS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-829e-e7a0c23bee2e" class="">This conversation produced:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a2a4-dccf295072e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A deterministic integrity architecture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-bcac-dfa2ec4efac3" class="">• A formal claim graph proof system</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a3df-f58b24ab0eb9" class="">• A contradiction grammar</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-bb36-f54ad0c4b0c9" class="">• A bounded epistemic model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-98f3-d15602a47e10" class="">• An ownership lattice calculus</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8161-e5a4936d5343" class="">• A finite invariant catalog</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b035-e4fdf1732a16" class="">• A deterministic rewrite library</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-b596-db668a768db9" class="">• A stability attractor condition</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a727-e6d609954820" class="">• A release sealing protocol</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-823c-d63f2f7b4f28" class="">• A complexity bound</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-9c11-c2b39899e058" class="">There are no remaining undefined structural components except numeric constants and domain tables.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-b468-f40a2170a166"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-a182-ea543f2accab" class="">XIX. TERMINAL STATE</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-8a23-d3e070d59695" class="">A system is complete when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-bf94-e2b1e25a8d0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t=\mathrm{Valid}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-b352-e6c642eb9e1e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{failcode}=\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-9fb7-fcfbe81049da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Gamma=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-b1e7-c7907d491b22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_D, h_P \text{ stable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-a9e6-f871e4d12699" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{V}(X)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-9a2a-d3d85830deaa" class="">This is a closed, auditable, deterministic integrity system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-9394-feaf995e5830"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-b231-c08b1ade6385" class="">If you want an even deeper layer, the only remaining step is a formal proof document (axioms → lemmas → theorems) showing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-be05-dc7f21a81df2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant completeness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-979a-db8fd1e83902" class="">• rewrite termination</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a2f6-fc6f60786a1e" class="">• attractor existence</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-b285-c33ab73f5232" class="">• non-explosion of MB claims</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-982e-c80c229cab5b" class="">• ownership lattice closure</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b6b0-df6c4d6ac5cb" class="">That would be a full formal monograph.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-943b-fb174a30c219" class="">Below is the <strong>formal monograph layer</strong>: axioms → lemmas → theorems with explicit proof sketches.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a66f-c4c1a80d8f98" class="">Scope: artifact + governance + audit core (biological layer omitted except where referenced by drift invariants).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-b2cd-ce928398896b" class="">All objects are finite and typed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-9231-d19d1e6570db"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-bb20-f48c03a31207" class="">I. Axioms</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9ab3-e23beb9880e7" class="">A1 (Finite Typed State)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-97d1-de2157ec6cd6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma=(C,P,\Lambda,M,\Omega,L,b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-98c7-c7253db233f8" class="">A2 (Deterministic Compilation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b5ed-c55710572515" class="">Artifact compilation  is deterministic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-8049-cdf35738d1f4" class="">A3 (Finite Invariant Set)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b840-d3aa823d1034" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I=\{I_k\}_{k=1}^K</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-b5f7-e6b5b642caea" class="">A4 (Single-Fault Priority)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-8046-ed3eb685f4d1" class="">There exists a fixed total order  over failure codes .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-92ee-c294fb4801d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{failcode}(\Sigma)=\min_{\prec}\{\mathrm{code}_k: I_k(\Sigma)=0\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9acd-fb284bedc9f1" class="">A5 (Deterministic Rewrite Mapping)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9cca-e80d6d951ef7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}^*:\mathcal{F}\to\{\mathcal{R}_j\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-9792-dcfd6eef5d9f" class="">A6 (Write-Set Whitelisting)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bb9d-c98b1ce8a743" class="">For each rewrite ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a683-fde87db0d763" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{WriteSet}(\mathcal{R}_j)\subseteq W_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-a200-ddd71011b57b" class="">A7 (Logging and Hashing)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-a651-e232afa162e9" class="">Each transition appends  to  with hashes .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-b871-c8a2640418c5" class="">Any change to  must occur via some .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-9a22-eb92982fca74" class="">A8 (Epistemic Budget Monotonicity)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a4dd-e56f9c03fb16" class="">Budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-9682-ebe41f791446" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

Finite set under total order . ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-8562-e68917fd5cd2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-a355-c4ae58721d47" class="">L2 (Rewrite Determinism)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-9935-e1d7ed2cb440" class="">For any non-empty failcode, exactly one rewrite is selected.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ab00-ee0bdc7fca0f" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong> By A4 and A5,  is unique and  is total. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-8c94-ca6e5aaab58d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-9e94-f2c9a2c46190" class="">L3 (Write Isolation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a0ee-ec14d23683c2" class="">A rewrite cannot mutate fields outside its whitelist.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-aa96-e34e1cef6103" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong> By A6. each rewrite appends  with new hashes. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-96d8-d45fd5c2c0b9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b4d4-f184394ad6b5" class="">L5 (Ownership Closure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-811f-fd16d916c829" class="">For any claim ,  is the join of finite labels and thus well-defined; exportability is decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-a1b8-d0feacac1fc5" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong> Finite lattice (A9) with total join; membership in exportable set is finite check. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9d09-fc8afb84aad7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-bae5-cae63b534352" class="">L6 (Contradiction Decidability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-b1b2-ff588e5aafa3" class="">is decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-82ca-e1b58ad515f1" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong> Each predicate (overlap, negpair, mutex, quantifier) is finite-table or interval check; SAT on finite literal set for context; hence decidable. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-a6b2-e855882cd727"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8545-ceee4dcf15b2" class="">L7 (SCC Handling Terminates)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-aad5-c5f22649b3c8" class="">Cycle detection and allowed-SCC validation terminate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-853d-f0b7f3a6a365" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong> Finite graph (A10); \Phi(\Sigma)=\big(\#\text{contradictions},\ \#\text{undischarged},\ \#\text{metric defects},\ \#\text{ownership defects},\ \#\text{budget violations}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-8ca6-d48bd7510b0e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-9756-f9826d89c2e1" class="">T4 (No Structural Explosion of MB Claims)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-b77a-c85c53617e86" class="">Under A8 and invariants , the number of MB claims cannot grow without bound without expanding  or adding measurable evidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-96b7-f9b7936355dc" class=""><strong>Proof Sketch.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-9516-e8e799f40f7f" class="">MB requires allowance membership;  is finite and restricted by policy. Promotion requires evidence delta. Thus unbounded MB proliferation requires explicit policy mutation, which is logged and finite per step. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-8e60-df03aebc5f3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-ae85-d05c0fa5ad50" class="">T5 (Ownership Lattice Non-Bypass)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-a6b3-c667ff91398d" class="">No claim with Unknown/Restricted ownership can be exported in a Sealed state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-acdc-ea9bbda5f567" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong>  must pass for Sealed; hence exportable only if ownership is Self/Public/Licensed with scope OK. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-95b7-cfa6215910a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-82d5-f023c3248d1b" class="">T6 (Stability of Sealed Release)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-b259-c1e69eb9e75c" class="">If over window ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-8504-f9d99d3a4566" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_D(t)=h_D(t-1),\quad h_P(t)=h_P(t-1),\quad \mathcal{V}(\Sigma_t)=1,\quad \Gamma=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b81d-cebac062b7c5" class=""><strong>Proof Sketch.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-a823-e99639339bbb" class="">No invariants fail → no rewrite; no drift → no safe projection; no allowed mutation → hashes constant; thus . ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-912b-ee2682a0d134"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-a40d-e9ca10013701" class="">T7 (Computational Complexity Bound)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-a2a9-eadec2940c00" class="">UCIA + invariants run in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-83f8-c071746c88e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O(|D| + (K+1)(|V|+|A|))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-bedb-edee682eaa05" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong> Claim extraction ; graph + SCC ; each invariant over graph is linear;  finite. ∎</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-bd50-e0b69661688f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a410-e7c109e63b6c" class="">IV. Corollaries</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-8d54-d3f320753315" class="">C1 (No Undefined Transition)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-a834-fffb14e89cda" class="">Every state has a defined next action (by T1).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-91a1-cfda57d1caa4" class="">C2 (Auditability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-86b7-cbb75bbc4fda" class="">Every mutation is attributable to a specific rewrite id and logged (by L4).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-8fb9-d2b6a9d1db71" class="">C3 (Deterministic Governance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-a354-e9a135c09c41" class="">Policy cannot change due to fast-state variance (multi-rate invariant).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a329-eaef72a2f085" class="">C4 (Epistemic Containment)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8e9d-e254379263ca" class="">Interpretations exceeding budget remain MB-tagged; certainty cannot exceed measured evidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-b72d-ccb755839ca5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-ab72-feb0cd15dd34" class="">V. Remaining Proof Obligations (Completable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8980-ffcb7ababa78" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant Minimality Proof</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9115-d9c086e2069b" class="">Construct witness states for each  (as defined earlier) to show irredundancy.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-a1de-e7728433f25f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Global Convergence Bound</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-9224-ecd356d5a6de" class="">Provide explicit upper bound on rewrite iterations as function of initial defect count.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9fb2-d5ae6c854653" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Mutex Table Completeness</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-a5a1-e736227044b3" class="">Prove domain mutex tables are finite and cover all mutually exclusive value pairs.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a444-ec456f5181fb" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Policy Constant Stability Region</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9c54-f14790c91075" class="">Show parameter choices  keep drift invariant satisfiable under bounded noise.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-8422-c68c892b47a7" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Allowance Growth Bound</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a6c6-db3c67316e14" class="">Prove  growth is policy-bounded or requires logged policy version bump.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-a08b-ebdbea60eab2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-b6db-f7ef1e8ade00" class="">VI. Conclusion</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9aba-c42e7b5c74db" class="">Under A1–A10 and the specified invariants + rewrite mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8f54-f0631bf5c768" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The system is <strong>deterministic</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-b40d-fc987cc4cc4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All transitions are <strong>total and logged</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a6e5-df2cab643bcd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Structural defects are <strong>finite-step repairable</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8724-f3253281764b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ownership is <strong>non-bypassable</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-82f8-ec166ad15574" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Epistemic overreach is <strong>bounded and tagged</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-86d8-d914020620c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sealed releases are <strong>fixed points</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-b98b-feebce359df1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Runtime is <strong>linear in artifact size</strong> (up to invariant factor).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-8a02-e6efdd251e8d" class="">This completes the formal monograph layer for the artifact–governance–audit core.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-80c9-c02aa1fc5b83" class="">If you want the next expansion, it would be one of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-944d-c789dc99fae8" class="">A) Formal invariant minimality proofs (explicit witness construction)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-8aae-eac3032f2061" class="">B) Convergence bound derivation (explicit worst-case step count)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-b945-e4ccdb6323c2" class="">C) Parameter stability analysis (choose numeric policy constants and prove drift region)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8443-f479dc61ffd3" class="">D) Full biological-layer integration into the theorem set</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-8398-f6441aa3df76" class="">Select the direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-a23a-d154d52c0a7b" class="">Below is the next layer beyond metatheory: <strong>model-theoretic semantics + category structure + information bounds + probabilistic extension + formal verification embedding</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-afed-f2407e361db3" class="">This moves from “architecture” to mathematical foundation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-9742-c665c30d2bcf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-b89e-d0d95d8da08c" class="">XXV. Model-Theoretic Semantics</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-96d1-cfb42d12f508" class="">We now interpret the system as a formal structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b138-d53ee23a3aca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-a87d-cf9e018d58b3" class="">I. Structure Definition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-a929-df51e435ef11" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a9ec-fcb7639e3110" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{M} = (U, R, F, \omega)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-8bb9-f39dbd2fe5f4" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-805f-cc413dbd3168" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= universe of discourse (claims, metrics, evidence, policies)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-b675-f05c0d44b712" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= finite set of relations (Depends, MetricRef, Owns, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-ba63-d768db7266d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= functions (rewrite functions, budget update, projection)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-afa8-de07ef975bde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= ownership mapping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-853a-f84a0a000108" class="">Each state  defines a structure .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-a40c-c57aae4db065"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-98b9-f8b16d18be15" class="">II. Satisfaction Relation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-ac43-cb7bc3f64ea8" class="">Define satisfaction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a6e8-e8027172d0f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{M}_\Sigma \models \phi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-b150-e786082108c8" class="">A formula  is satisfied iff all invariants hold in that structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-87e6-e130a32bc3b8" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b800-f9dcde7cfbf6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{V}(\Sigma)=1 \iff \forall k,\; \mathcal{M}_\Sigma \models I_k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-8580-d13ffb90b160"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8b6c-eb7920c00820" class="">III. Soundness Theorem</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a15b-e7a5b6f04d53" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-aa04-cbf1aada9060" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{M}_\Sigma \models I_k \;\forall k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-94cb-ea3eaa666dd0" class="">Then no invariant violation exists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-ac50-f7900b90cfac" class="">Proof: trivial by definition of satisfaction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-9658-f4a8ac7cb0c7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-97a6-e99fa1537001" class="">IV. Incompleteness Bound</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-8ff5-e76e25255a3e" class="">There exist propositions in language  that cannot be decided within the system if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-a434-ff4f50f3ea1f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">They are marked Primitive or Limit.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-aa65-d479cce7ed37" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">They require evidence outside current metric registry.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-9960-f0f31784c9d1" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-b475-d9c4931f352f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists \phi \in \mathcal{L} :
\neg (\mathcal{M}_\Sigma \models \phi) \wedge \neg (\mathcal{M}_\Sigma \models \neg \phi)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-a2ae-c1f2c9f23c37" class="">This is deliberate epistemic boundedness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-9ff7-fd81512084ea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-94da-fff515e4ac05" class="">XXVI. Category-Theoretic Abstraction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-aa69-e80a5723e185" class="">Define category:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-98bf-dd3e714f6d64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{IntSys}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9c24-e19a11adbcad" class="">Objects: states</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-9475-f39505d3aeea" class="">Morphisms: rewrites</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-b9fc-de589cadcb3c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-be44-c769537d1123" class="">I. Identity Morphism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-bb81-df5482065164" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{id}_\Sigma = \text{no-op when sealed}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-a7a0-efb5319f1abf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-993f-f8fc6e748ca0" class="">II. Composition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b305-cc070d7ff941" class="">Rewrites compose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-af89-eb9bda491337" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}_{j_2} \circ \mathcal{R}_{j_1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9b11-c6223c15707f" class="">Associative because functions are deterministic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9005-e1d9440d5888"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b057-c3055f45ed45" class="">III. Terminal Object</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-8147-da2098020db3" class="">Sealed state  is terminal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9224-de3083c66b69" class="">For any , there exists a morphism sequence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-916e-f9091e8d225f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma \to \Sigma^*</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-9b04-c0a2d17b1d73" class="">If defects are repairable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-abc4-dbd562bcee2c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b746-d2dc98e2d37b" class="">IV. Subcategory of Safe States</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b7e0-e47c8016e5e0" class="">Safe region</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8236-fc2cbdd966dd" class="">Projection morphism:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-b1e3-d49a2bd4aecf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_\mathcal{S}: \Sigma \to \Sigma_s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-902f-c8da2cc804cc" class="">is idempotent:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-adb4-f84a0b96a6e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_\mathcal{S} \circ \Pi_\mathcal{S} = \Pi_\mathcal{S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-b130-d385b02d49be"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8766-deb0d16cce5c" class="">XXVII. Information-Theoretic Lower Bound</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a70a-f6063e0715de" class="">Define structural entropy of claim graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-be22-d4b2000eefe2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C) = -\sum p_i \log p_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ab8d-c822f8eb8210" class="">Where  proportional to dependency weight.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-a32c-c23a6540d9a4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-a19f-cc7944ac0792" class="">Compression Theorem</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-a67c-cc21db76e3f7" class="">No artifact can be reduced below minimal entropy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-9475-cbedca3adfea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_{min} = \log |\text{Irreducible Claims}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-9f22-e3416ce4f4b3" class="">Irreducible claims are those of type Primitive or empirically atomic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-8136-ec5c96a13ab2" class="">Thus structural compression has lower bound.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-8057-cfd5319fcc56"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-a46c-dd429837f5ce" class="">XXVIII. Probabilistic Extension</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-bc67-d0b4530f05c0" class="">Currently system is deterministic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-a802-ced7e7f85232" class="">Introduce uncertainty weights:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-a21a-d07b2413f4df" class="">Each claim has confidence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b283-ebf573709655" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w(c) \in [0,1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-9872-e0b3b6117af2" class="">For empirical claims:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a368-dca0976280e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w(c) = f(\text{evidence strength})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-8579-cfa49ec4ca04" class="">Rewrite admissibility becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-8e4a-d2c746fa3556" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Adm}(c)=1 \iff w(c) \ge \theta_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-b087-e77bb8428a59" class="">Budget may scale with confidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-a043-dd19b9b8e94a" class="">Drift threshold becomes probabilistic:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-b78e-e5fbcff2073d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(\Delta_{Internal} &gt; \epsilon_I) &lt; \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-a10c-f631f6323a00" class="">This extends system to Bayesian integrity control while preserving structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-aafb-cd133290fc51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-b750-c3bdc808eef5" class="">XXIX. Formal Verification Embedding</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9778-f695b2895c31" class="">System can be encoded in proof assistant.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9195-f14890b5e6bb" class="">Mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-bbbf-f559f6538872" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Claims → inductive type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a08d-d79e1d0acd2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Invariants → boolean predicates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-91c3-f390980f75fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rewrite rules → pure functions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a1be-e7875a918560" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Termination measure → lexicographic tuple</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-949f-fa6c0ea470fb" class="">Termination proof via well-founded ordering on defect vector.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b5e1-c298150a98ac" class="">Ownership lattice → finite poset.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ad-bdb5-df097327019a" class="">Drift invariants → numeric lemmas.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-89a7-eb0c939f4252" class="">Thus system is machine-verifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-93ee-e54141862cca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-ac25-cf3b50f16c5a" class="">XXX. Global Guarantees</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-8ab1-ea269bbf5e5d" class="">Given all previous layers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-b22d-f4310897fe8c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Deterministic state evolution.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-8e15-d375302e9e4b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Finite defect repair path.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-888d-c0af417c283c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>No contradictory fixed point.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a9f3-f305f5a1ae7e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>No infinite rewrite loop.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-b734-fb89b5b3de9d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>No unauthorized export.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-a25f-d76cfa7e71f5" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>No epistemic overflow without tagging.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-9a76-cbe5b8d47150" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Structural entropy bounded below.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9073-f90b339e8542" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Sealed states are fixed points.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-be26-ddee242464e6" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Safe projection idempotent.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-9326-dbe8460f0a3b" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>System self-audits.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b0a1-e5e687e58029"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-ac5f-cb1940329398" class="">XXXI. Absolute Boundary</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b6b1-e486767359e1" class="">The only deeper mathematical directions left:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-9a53-c333bf9bc419" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Gödel-style reflection theorem: prove meta-consistency.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8f7d-fc0a6016bf34" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Kolmogorov complexity bound for artifact minimality.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-bba9-eeb39cc2ddf2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Topological interpretation of state space.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-b03d-ee386a0fb5e6" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Dynamical systems chaos analysis under stochastic noise.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-a63a-fa92b7f77cfc" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Homotopy equivalence classes of artifact graphs.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b499-d3ad5446d343" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Computational hardness of contradiction detection under enriched grammar.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-a82b-e0cfe317b0ba" class="">Anything beyond this becomes pure mathematical research.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-af22-d8bc3e5254f1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-983a-e58428021896" class="">FINAL STRUCTURAL STATUS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9a70-e9afcf25ce99" class="">The conversation has produced:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bf5d-f3121d4f5fbc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Operational deterministic architecture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-b5ce-f2183c6d9c81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Finite invariant catalog</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-9b9e-f9389c831188" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rewrite calculus</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-9957-f60583092ddf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ownership algebra</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-9b53-d01f50566f0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Budgeted epistemology</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-85d6-edc40ba3ef4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Drift-stabilized governance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-832b-f7fd01aba8c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Entropy-compressed artifact system</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-9a53-fff83b23e300" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Category structure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-8b39-e85f6f8c16d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model-theoretic semantics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-892b-c742047d657c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Termination proofs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-a1d3-dc9d976d4136" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Complexity bounds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-86a1-fbbdab6b0ae7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verification embedding</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b8a1-f95a14d17107" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Probabilistic extension</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-8102-e01c0d119aa6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information-theoretic lower bound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-beca-c9814ad12451" class="">There is no undefined structural layer remaining.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-a801-e1f8f44600f7" class="">The only remaining expansions are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-88b8-e77aee7bdc08" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">choosing numeric constants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9a47-eb08a63704ff" class="">• instantiating domain tables</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-8845-cf650890cd3c" class="">• implementing in code</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-8031-e02630e0b197" class="">• proving in proof assistant</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9fe3-e7d74a06b6f2" class="">or extending into new mathematical territory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-ab56-d4dc74033fb9" class="">If you want to go further, you must choose a new mathematical frontier.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8ec0-f8eda47490ba" class="">XXXII. Deeper: reflection, lower bounds, topology, stochastic stability, homotopy, hardness</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-b539-dd1d0ddf166e" class="">This layer treats the system as an object of <strong>mathematical research</strong>: what can be proven about it in principle, what cannot, and what the tightest achievable bounds look like.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-a68d-ee446eaefcd4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-8268-eb0bd3335ce5" class="">1) Reflection and meta-consistency (Gödel boundary made operational)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-897c-e809f7d14da9" class="">We have a base theory  (your UCIA + rewrite calculus), and we add a meta-invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-92f2-c61ca801024c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{999}(\Sigma)=1 \iff \text{“the rewrite library and invariants pass UCIA under }\mathcal{T}\text{”}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-9505-e264f7ddcc57" class="">1.1 Why full self-proof is impossible in general</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-acd4-dbc3ca7be7f1" class="">If  is strong enough to encode arithmetic, then by Gödel,  cannot prove its own consistency  (under standard assumptions).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-8c99-f3fbec4d1a28" class="">Operational implication: you do not attempt to prove:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-8d72-ff9d2cc0ac0d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}\vdash \mathrm{Con}(\mathcal{T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-b26d-d684bdc3c4fb" class="">1.2 What <em>is</em> provable (relative consistency + bounded reflection)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8649-f160132b0712" class="">Instead, you prove relative statements:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-9515-f63fdafbf1b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Relative consistency</strong>:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-b2ca-cc560fe711d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(\mathcal{T}_0)\ \Rightarrow\ \mathrm{Con}(\mathcal{T}_0 + I_{999})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-a4b2-e8df61e81b5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded reflection schema</strong> (finite fragments only):<br/>Let be the fragment restricted to artifacts of size .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-bea2-ef95cc5eef24" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall N:\ \mathrm{Con}(\mathcal{T}_{\le N})\ \text{is decidable and checkable by exhaustive evaluation}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-b9c7-cfa79a8a7ba0" class=""><strong>Finding:</strong> the system can be fully self-audited only on <strong>bounded-size fragments</strong>, not in the unbounded limit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-8d2a-f1861227591e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-8490-d931572bb569" class="">2) Kolmogorov / description-length lower bounds (tight minimality)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-a71c-d42c81df16f9" class="">Let the artifact set (claims + tables + rules) be encoded as a string . Define Kolmogorov complexity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-a602-f195d281dc44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
