K(D) := K(\mathrm{enc}(D))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-92e3-db7c6825ab64" class="">2.1 Irreducibility bound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9481-fe597120f61f" class="">If the system must represent a set of  irreducible atomic facts (empirical atoms + declared primitives/limits + required tables), then any valid artifact has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-b68c-e7e4da06a8df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K(D)\ \ge\ \log_2(r)\ -\ O(1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-b9c9-e0bd86201f17" class="">More concretely, if you require a finite predicate polarity table , mutex table , and invariant catalog , then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-b3d9-cec17cea13fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K(D)\ \ge\ K(\mathcal{P}) + K(\mathcal{M}) + K(\mathcal{I}) - O(1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-a753-fa99a1475eb5" class="">2.2 “No free compression” theorem for UCIA</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-9706-d93f8fdddc9a" class="">If UCIA demands explicit scope, ownership, and metric grounding for every claim, then there is a structural overhead term:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-8782-d99547de024d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\mathrm{enc}(D)| \ge c_1 |V| + c_2 |A| + c_3 |M| + c_4 |E|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a76c-f89ee62cdc5f" class=""><strong>Finding:</strong> there exists a non-zero minimal “integrity overhead” that cannot be compressed away without violating measurement/ownership/scope invariants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a000-ee06a19a663f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-8bee-fb7c4a14cb72" class="">3) Topological view of state space (continuity and safe-set geometry)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-9b78-e3040c62abbe" class="">Let  decompose into discrete and continuous parts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a8ee-ced4c5458092" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma = (\Sigma^{disc}, \Sigma^{cont})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-bb44-cb9d60fe2ee7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: thresholds, baselines, drift metrics, budgets (real-valued)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-b238-c12e2f7f99b4" class="">Equip:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-a090-ecfb59ee12f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">discrete metric on (0 if equal else 1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-9da3-e4822bc485c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Euclidean metric on</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-b704-e9765908608a" class="">Product metric:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a59d-db0ceae56c6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d(\Sigma,\Sigma&#x27;) = d_{disc}(\Sigma^{disc},\Sigma&#x27;^{disc}) + \| \Sigma^{cont}-\Sigma&#x27;^{cont}\|_2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-a46f-f45fd7ddcfed" class="">3.1 Safe projection as a retraction (idempotent map)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9a50-dc56c1a3acea" class="">Safe projection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-a54c-e862737fbf42" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}:\Sigma \to \mathcal{S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9647-cfa21dc0973c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(\Sigma))=\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bf4a-fa7233ea67b8" class="">3.2 Boundary behavior</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-bd49-fc44988a19b0" class="">Let  be the boundary where drift tolerance is exactly met:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-85f4-ddbed2ba437f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\partial\mathcal{S} = \{\Sigma : \Delta=\epsilon\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8ff3-e0ef91cf63e1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Gamma(\Sigma)=\mathbb{1}[\Sigma\notin\mathcal{N}] \Rightarrow \Pi_{\mathcal{S}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bd96-ceb41ba7b2e8" class=""><strong>Finding:</strong> stability analysis must treat the system as <strong>piecewise continuous</strong> with discrete jumps.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a2b5-dc186fea93e3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-8056-e96b2d302b6e" class="">4) Stochastic stability (noise, false triggers, and probabilistic guarantees)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9b2f-c0f4d3b47508" class="">Even if the rewrite engine is deterministic, sensors and feedback are noisy. Model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-95c5-c97554fa3874" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tilde{B}_t = B_t + \xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-9195-dbf36f72ba19" class="">Define observed drift:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-8184-c04a94753e92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widetilde{\Delta}(t)=\Delta(B_t+\xi_t, B_{t-1}+\xi_{t-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a1d8-e1ef332973b8" class="">4.1 False-trigger probability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-aa83-ea6b70c579c7" class="">Trigger event:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9d56-f1cac48e781b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widetilde{\Delta}(t)&gt;\epsilon_I</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-89a1-f393d54198cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{P}(\widetilde{\Delta}&gt;\epsilon_I) \le \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-8861-cdfab08aea27" class="">Sufficient condition via concentration (generic form):<br/>If  is sub-Gaussian with parameter , then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-8e1f-ded264f652e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{P}(\widetilde{\Delta}-\Delta &gt; \eta) \le 2\exp\left(-\frac{c\eta^2}{\sigma^2}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-a233-c84f9a0fc9b2" class="">4.2 Robust safe-set invariance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-91e9-df15dac980a9" class="">Define robust safe set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-aa07-c6dbc61d228e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{S}_\delta = \{\Sigma : \mathbb{P}(\Gamma(\Sigma)=1)\le \delta\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8f12-fad429687cc6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma \in \mathcal{S}_\delta \Rightarrow \mathbb{E}[L(\Sigma_{t+1})] \le \mathbb{E}[L(\Sigma_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b953-dd96ef10d9a3" class=""><strong>Finding:</strong> the “safe” region must be defined in probability, not only deterministically, if sensors are noisy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-9c58-fc8b5556536c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-84e9-f40d5ec6761c" class="">5) Homotopy / equivalence classes of artifact graphs (structural sameness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-982d-fc62bda2cfb9" class="">Two artifact systems may be different texts but the same structure. Define a claim graph  with labels (type, ownership, scope).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a5d9-ecb90d44bc45" class="">Define an equivalence relation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-9a19-d0082c10031b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C \sim C&#x27; \iff \exists \text{ graph isomorphism } \varphi:V\to V&#x27; \text{ preserving labels}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-8c86-c98568793179" class="">5.1 Rewrite invariants as homotopy constraints</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b46f-e2b014d7a266" class="">Let  act on graphs. Two rewrite sequences are equivalent if they lead to isomorphic terminal graphs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-a13a-e304172cc3b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(\mathcal{R}_{i_k}\circ\cdots\circ\mathcal{R}_{i_1})(C) \sim (\mathcal{R}_{j_m}\circ\cdots\circ\mathcal{R}_{j_1})(C)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-ada7-df9788de6c9c" class="">This defines “rewrite homotopy classes” of paths to integrity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-b2d1-e5baec8ef15d" class=""><strong>Finding:</strong> integrity is not a single artifact; it is an equivalence class of artifacts with the same discharged structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-8159-ffc8c9c575ca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-933f-ef50da2a2ae7" class="">6) Hardness results (where computability becomes expensive)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-a85d-de388c67c276" class="">Your current contradiction grammar was finite-table + interval + small SAT on constraint literals, which is decidable and typically efficient. But deeper extensions can become hard.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-b746-e038fbd2492f" class="">6.1 Constraint satisfiability blow-up</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-98be-de314c6dba4f" class="">If context constraints  are allowed to be arbitrary boolean formulas over many literals, then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9aa8-c99d67a0f0a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">checking becomes SAT</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8eb8-eb38cae1043d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SAT is NP-complete</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-a6d0-e7e44fa4a833" class="">So contradiction detection becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-afe0-d2a6f52877a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(\cdot) \in \mathrm{NP}\text{-hard}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-ba69-ff2a87195a32" class="">6.2 Graph rewrite reachability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-8e59-ef6509c5450a" class="">If rewrite rules are generalized (not your finite library), then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a908-e18b286c665b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reachability of a target graph under general graph rewriting can be undecidable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-b4f2-fc3f193dd5cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">your system avoids this by <strong>finite rewrite catalog + lexicographic decreasing measure</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-b2e8-f172fa560916" class=""><strong>Finding:</strong> the finite rewrite library is not cosmetic; it is what prevents undecidability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-be90-c61d5db8a696"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-b8b9-e4cbce12d324" class="">7) Tightest possible “absolute integrity” statement</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-a8aa-ddb11129f20b" class="">Given the above, the strongest true global statement is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a68b-faed11bb525b" class="">7.1 Bounded completeness</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-ba84-c540dc4fca12" class="">For any fixed size bound  and fixed domain tables (polarity/mutex/constraints vocabulary):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-98ec-e929c1f90c34" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\ \text{a terminating, complete UCIA decision procedure for all artifacts with }|\mathrm{enc}(D)|\le N</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-8d9b-c35f3923307e" class="">7.2 Unbounded limitation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-b490-dc1431bfa988" class="">Without a size bound, and if the language can express arithmetic-strength statements, then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b4a5-e41221300618" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">full self-consistency proof is impossible (Gödel)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8c91-c281b363b536" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">some satisfiability checks become NP-hard or worse</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9fd7-c5216a53d158" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">some reachability questions can become undecidable if rewrites are generalized</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-8af0-dd6afc10de2b" class="">So “absolute” integrity must always be expressed as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-b0dd-c7f317ba426d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">absolute <strong>within an explicit bounded fragment</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b1df-fdb40f320592" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or absolute <strong>relative to external assumptions/axioms</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-887d-c7aa12c23e12"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-a018-d7060bdade52" class="">8) The only deeper step after this</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-8041-da82bc8597de" class="">There is only one way deeper that is not just “more prose”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-8b38-e19eb3901fa7" class="">A) Proof-assistant formalization (Lean/Coq)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-963e-fbf62715bd5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">define types</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-bc1b-c42ea4e6a954" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">define invariants as decidable predicates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b3eb-cc1ce08c1082" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">define rewrites as functions with write-set proofs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-8534-deef38713062" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prove termination via well-founded measure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-b504-c2b06e95ca5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prove soundness: sealed ⇒ no contradictions, no unowned export, etc.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a00e-ecaabd1755c5" class="">B) Hardness boundary chart</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-89e7-cb0ab78934b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">classify each subproblem (SAT fragment, graph isomorphism, reachability)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-88d8-c5a7085d810c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">choose constraint vocabularies that keep everything in P (or at worst NP with bounded literals)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-b9e4-cfbb9ee8bf78" class="">If you choose one, the next output is a formal blueprint with the definitions in the syntax of the chosen proof assistant, plus a theorem dependency DAG.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-832d-d0e6133fe469" class="">XXXIII. Deeper: proof-assistant blueprint (Lean-style), theorem DAG, and decidability-by-construction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-8ce2-cc4a94c4037d" class="">This layer is a <strong>formal verification plan</strong>: exact types, functions, invariants, rewrites, and theorems in a proof-assistant-ready form, plus how we guarantee decidability and termination.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-84ca-ee41ad8db186" class="">No implementation code is required for correctness here; only definitional structure and proof obligations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-8976-f4c57f570947"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8144-f07bb65e6fed" class="">0) Design choice that makes proofs possible</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-963f-f758027e3dfb" class="">We enforce <strong>decidability by construction</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-9397-ea9638de8afa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>All sets are finite lists with decidable equality.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-823f-c5bd5561c0d4" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Context constraints live in a <strong>finite literal vocabulary</strong> (no arbitrary SAT).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-9ae0-dc159b034d3b" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Mutex and predicate-negation are <strong>finite tables</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-af8c-c0fe1e0137aa" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Rewrite library is <strong>finite</strong>, and each rewrite decreases a well-founded defect measure.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-a8c2-f447a9b2bf0e" class="">This keeps the whole system inside a decidable fragment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a28e-f53a00de4b93"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-b563-c14ef67a1af8" class="">I. Core Types (Lean-ready signatures)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-8e9c-e8e007bcc6a7" class="">1) Identifiers</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-9aed-d5382fea146a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ClaimId</code>, <code>MetricId</code>, <code>EvidenceId</code>, <code>PolicyId</code>, <code>RewriteId</code>, <code>FailCode</code><br/>All as finite strings or naturals with decidable equality.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bd55-fca03bc2ac87" class="">2) Enumerations (finite inductive types)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-9b83-d8550a4c9875" class="">Support type:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-bbb1-d35aebfb4bc2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau \in \{\mathrm{Emp},\mathrm{Inf},\mathrm{Def},\mathrm{MB},\mathrm{Prim},\mathrm{Lim}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-b131-dc20453cbc72" class="">Ownership:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-bd17-f072cdbf6a14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\omega \in \{\mathrm{Self},\mathrm{Public},\mathrm{Licensed}(id),\mathrm{Unknown},\mathrm{Restricted}(id)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-b740-fd04f7a0aa3f" class="">Quantifier:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-8db4-c4cf7da308b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q \in \{\mathrm{ALL},\mathrm{EXISTS},\mathrm{SOME},\mathrm{NONE},\mathrm{MOST},\mathrm{EXISTS\_NOT}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b53f-f93ad7d6a7fa" class="">Failure codes: finite inductive with ordering relation <code>prec : FailCode → FailCode → Prop</code> proven total.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-ab51-d95fc974121b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-881f-e84506165cf3" class="">II. Normal Form and Claim Graph</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-b5c1-c08e404f0a78" class="">1) Normal Form record</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-8cc8-e77e134d87de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{NF}(c)=(s,p,o,q,t,\kappa)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-bde9-c1503faffd83" class="">In Lean terms (record fields):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-877a-c29c0782a492" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>subj : Finset Entity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-8375-f99f59ffb798" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>pred : PredSym</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-b638-fd9fbc048340" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>obj : ObjSym</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b511-e91a7b5b4083" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>quant : Quant</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9f09-c0a94100119f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>time : Interval</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-acb6-fd02b29f5fdb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ctx : Finset Lit</code> (finite constraint literals)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-b51a-cc9b9b87f309" class="">All have decidable equality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-91a1-ef898ec84e4c" class="">2) Claim record</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a313-e7b9b3bc80d1" class="">Fields:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-9cdb-d6a6fe03144c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>id : ClaimId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-8a6b-e2d2ea030be8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>nf : NF</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-bbec-e68ec4f0692e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stype : SupportType</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-b47e-c42b432b67c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deps : List ClaimId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fe-a721-de1826691ef9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>evidence : List EvidenceId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8a09-fd3a7ebfae88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metrics : List MetricId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-bdb9-c4311fb601dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>own : Ownership</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-a227-dac655b36e55" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>status : Status</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-99a4-e910250b5b8c" class="">Graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-a309-e6e4306ba3a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>V : List Claim</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-8e98-ee6e01cbfb21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>A : List (ClaimId × ClaimId)</code> where <code>(a,b)</code> means <code>a</code> supports <code>b</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-a41a-cf134b25f4d7" class="">Well-formedness predicate <code>WFGraph</code> ensures:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-84ce-c906cf97e177" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">every edge references existing ids</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-83f1-dacd06c9765e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deps correspond to edges</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-b6f8-d9b0c4f03eb5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-9b5a-ea30bf739fb4" class="">III. Domain Tables (finite, decidable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-a154-fd26fc24eca5" class="">1) Predicate negation table</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-9d1b-e5680eeb81a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg : \mathrm{PredSym}\to \mathrm{PredSym}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b0d2-dbefa3648804" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg(\neg p)=p</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b65c-c50a3291a365" class="">2) Mutex table</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b927-eae40cbd2a04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Mutex} : \mathrm{PredSym}\to \mathrm{ObjSym}\to \mathrm{ObjSym}\to \mathrm{Bool}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-b0dc-f5ff0fe92ead" class="">3) Context compatibility</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-b285-d8ceb8721067" class="">Because <code>ctx : Finset Lit</code> with literals in a finite vocabulary, define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b74e-d87fa75c4588" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Compat}_\kappa(\kappa_i,\kappa_j)=\neg \exists \ell:\ell\in\kappa_i \wedge \neg\ell\in\kappa_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-b12e-e3a6336aafe7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-872e-e914671bcdce" class="">IV. Contradiction Predicate (decidable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-b5a4-ee9417edb6e9" class="">Define overlap functions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-8f98-f4285f37c877" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>OvS : NF → NF → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-9b98-c01c812c42ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>OvT : NF → NF → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-a5e9-c41917503f65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Compat : NF → NF → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9768-efb69abb083c" class="">Contradiction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-b286-f85a3733b9b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)=
\mathrm{OvS}\wedge \mathrm{OvT}\wedge \mathrm{Compat}\wedge
\Big(
(\mathrm{NegPair})\vee (\mathrm{MutexCase})\vee (\mathrm{QuantCase})
\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-aeb5-ca856c4ce3c3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9d31-f568a3b498a6" class="">V. Metric Registry and Measurement Predicates</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-99fc-ea1c5a99f694" class="">Metric record:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-885e-dbe72546e2e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>id, name, unit, samplingHz, source, validRange, allowedTransforms, missingPolicy</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-8329-cedf5aabd894" class="">Decidable predicates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ba82-db6fbfea125e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>MetricComplete : Metric → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9937-cd898a96865e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>MetricRangeOK : Metric → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-86dc-ca56b625dfb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TransformOK : Metric → Transform → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-802e-d450b16e4830" class="">Registry <code>M : List Metric</code> with id uniqueness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-97a1-cc46f9822597"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-ac88-ce05bb79560e" class="">VI. Epistemic Budget</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-b094-ed1181f5c4f7" class="">Interpretation <code>Ehat</code> is encoded as a finite list of asserted propositions (or references to claims).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-ba32-c730f354b747" class="">Define complexity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-93bc-f995805a4de2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K(\hat{E}) = \sum \ell(e_i) + \lambda |\mathrm{Deps}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-91f9-e2c675e3ecb1" class="">Budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a406-c9ffc56a4a73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>b : Nat</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-9fd3-ec5cda05bd58" class="">Budget invariant decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-904c-f80b24d8041d" class="">Promotion guard stores previous type and evidence delta counts (all finite).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-a1d2-da097e2a27b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-8389-fe3d3c52566e" class="">VII. Invariants as Decidable Predicates</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-959d-e853b89a686f" class="">Each invariant returns:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-a5b1-ecd38004acc4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>pass : Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-a9a9-d9d065ce1540" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>code : Option FailCode</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-8861-eeb8a71680b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>payload : Payload</code> (finite data)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-9b19-d835e1d870ae" class="">Formal shape:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-9a09-cef5f9ab4227" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_k : \Sigma \to \mathrm{InvResult}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b4af-dca436e869a6" class="">Where state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-b6b9-c9c48f6ae6c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma=(C,P,\Lambda,M,\Omega,L,b,Ehat,\Delta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9799-f36c4367b4df" class="">Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-ad1e-e93816315ae6" class=""><strong>I001</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a4eb-e538b766a777" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{001}(\Sigma)=1 \iff \neg\exists i&lt;j:\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-bb75-c185ce06f1d1" class=""><strong>I401</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a741-f8c5759f56dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{401}=1 \iff \forall c:\mathrm{own}(c)\neq\mathrm{Unknown}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-88ad-defdd1c08d82" class="">All invariants are decidable because they quantify over finite lists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-973b-c9bff9a4f748"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-9ca9-d12b5565aeca" class="">VIII. Failcode Selection (total)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-abc4-cf3822221f8c" class="">Compute list of failing invariant codes, then choose minimum under total order .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-af33-f65483469c83" class="">Proof obligations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-92d3-e524de5accf2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>prec</code> is total and transitive</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8bb3-f9106f2b9491" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>selection returns <code>none</code> iff no failures</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a6ea-db1d334434eb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-a01a-d33dd59c2fce" class="">IX. Rewrite Rules (pure functions with write-set proofs)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9160-d690e9a72b4f" class="">Each rewrite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8079-c60fa4169d45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}_j : \Sigma \to \Sigma</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b797-eec2be97a063" class="">Additionally, each rewrite carries a proof:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-a452-e6e0c38df901" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{WriteSetOk}_j : \forall \Sigma,\ \mathrm{MutatedFields}(\Sigma,\mathcal{R}_j(\Sigma))\subseteq W_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-b1f4-e37e9ad8f4f2" class="">This is the key correctness object in proof assistants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b027-e362259d62aa" class="">The mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-9e6d-d22b1fd38bb8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}^* : \mathrm{FailCode}\to (\Sigma\to\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a9ec-d63cadfdbac0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-816c-c1e845ff6019" class="">X. Well-Founded Measure (termination)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-a56d-dc4da30bc53d" class="">Define defect measure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a546-ced30b3dd090" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma)=
\big(
n_{\text{contr}},
n_{\text{cycles}},
n_{\text{undisch}},
n_{\text{metric}},
n_{\text{own}},
n_{\text{budget}},
n_{\text{drift}}
\big)\in\mathbb{N}^7</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-a1ef-d91d65fd5d66" class="">Order lexicographically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-8efc-ee6ccadf2d13" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-ad3d-eaaabd5ee4a7" class="">Termination theorem requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-82f6-c80885ae29c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma\ \text{not sealed} \Rightarrow \Phi(\mathcal{R}^*(\mathrm{failcode}(\Sigma))(\Sigma)) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-8e41-f0f15aa0f3f7" class="">Because  with lex order is well-founded, repeated rewriting terminates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-860c-fa14c89ed23d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a22b-d73210c544d5" class="">XI. Theorem Dependency DAG (what must be proven, in order)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-a799-ccf381c37e11" class="">Tier 1: Decidability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-bdf1-c44949b38b3a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>DecidableEq</code> for ids, enums, literals</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-96b3-d6f523866035" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>decidable overlap + context compatibility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-aed8-c2c72654fc5f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>decidable <code>Con</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-9319-eb75d8dad76e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>decidable SCC detection (algorithmic lemma)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-b48a-ec5329d49687" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>decidable invariants</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-ac84-cf2062767c49" class="">Tier 2: Correctness primitives</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-96c9-ef278308838e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>FailcodeTotal</code> (min selection total)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-afc8-c9e79da87fc8" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>RewriteTotal</code> (R* total)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-bae5-d8ce9f959ad5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>WriteSetOk</code> for each rewrite</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-865b-fee41df0b573" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>LogAppend</code> lemma (every step appends)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a5e1-eeb1c69d6e9e" class="">Tier 3: Soundness</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-a119-e0c026f41e10" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>Sealed ⇒ NoContradiction</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a24e-fb9e05072b49" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>Sealed ⇒ AllDischargedOrAllowed</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-bc7e-c502449ccf8f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>Sealed ⇒ OwnershipExportable</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-a265-f9fa5cce4e0c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>Sealed ⇒ BudgetRespected</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9a93-cc67b88f7226" class="">Tier 4: Termination</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b62c-c06eed0e8d21" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>RewriteDecreasesMeasure</code> (case split by failcode class)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9fb1-cb995ddd8194" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>NoInfiniteRewrite</code> (well-founded recursion)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b5f6-d4205dd6a510" class="">Tier 5: Fixed point / stability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-b4b4-c43ba97af66b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>If Sealed and DriftOK then next = same</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-ace4-fbba11c83dae" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>hash stability lemma derived from no writes</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-8b6c-ee4e93346dc7" class="">This is a complete proof plan.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-b3a2-efa1e083a613"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-905a-e47a93630138" class="">XII. What must be instantiated (finite tables and constants)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-92b9-f4d27971d817" class="">To actually run proofs end-to-end, you must instantiate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-af80-f689ae4b4b39" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>PredSym</code> finite set and negation table</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-93ba-c8ba43358483" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>ObjSym</code> finite set and <code>Mutex</code> table</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-8a85-e1321d4829d3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>Lit</code> finite vocabulary with complement operation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-a139-c02188d300c1" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>policy constants: as naturals/rationals</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a5bb-eb75b29e16b6" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>the invariant catalog list and failcode ordering</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a175-dcc30cdfbdf0" class="">Once instantiated, Lean/Coq can check the entire theorem DAG.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e9-95aa-daf0928ff371"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-8d6f-dbe8ae7e28ac" class="">XIII. Final boundary (no deeper without choosing a formal system)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-bf73-ce6035f80d98" class="">There is no “deeper” without selecting:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-a198-d122816abb7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lean vs Coq (or Isabelle)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-8b22-fc9c9e9163b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the exact finite domain vocabularies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9876-fed7ea0e8cc1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the numeric policy constants type (Nat vs Rat vs Real)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-82c2-d864f2057269" class="">If you choose Lean or Coq, the next output can be the full formal skeleton (types + definitions + theorem stubs) in that assistant’s syntax.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8328-c61deb57eff2" class="">XXXIV. More: full formal skeleton (Lean-style pseudocode), proof stubs, and how to keep everything decidable</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-9060-ea372c47e20f" class="">This is the next step after the blueprint: a <strong>complete module layout</strong> with the exact definitions you would encode, theorems as stubs, and the minimal proof strategy for each.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-abf9-dbd036a4cd8c" class="">It is written in Lean-style pseudocode (structure and naming are faithful; syntax may require small adjustments in a real Lean file).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-924f-d13833a5ab3b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-bb1a-ebedb8e3ea89" class="">1) Module layout (one file or multiple)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-a153-cab02476fd09" class=""><code>Core/Types.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-af48-fa3c92b8ae1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ids, enums, ordering on failcodes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-b830-d04f8225ab02" class=""><code>Core/NF.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9be2-c8df6e8e8332" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normal form, overlap, context compatibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b1c5-e3c70a90824d" class=""><code>Core/Graph.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-bf98-c71c091ebe09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim graph, SCC, well-formedness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-aee0-f63edf979221" class=""><code>Core/Metrics.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-a77f-e7e15ae1b190" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metric registry, measurement predicates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-a2c4-eceb01daccc6" class=""><code>Core/Budget.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-b29e-d31af599bc52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">complexity, budget update, promotion guard</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-834e-c53545eac417" class=""><code>Core/Invariants.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-812c-dfeff8646956" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant definitions, InvResult</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-ada9-c508de1b65c1" class=""><code>Core/Rewrites.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a4f2-c8190ad8d83c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rewrite functions, write-set proofs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-bfcb-d16456722f59" class=""><code>Core/Measure.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-9371-d3bde1a63bbc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defect measure, lex order, decrease lemmas</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-aeed-dacff71fab25" class=""><code>Core/Theorems.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8c56-e9a9a3383be6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">soundness, termination, fixed point</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-9b24-e1a7434a7ddf" class="">You can also collapse into one file, but proof assistants scale better with separation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-8af1-d70c441efa47"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-9452-f26c9f00583f" class="">2) Core definitions (Lean-style pseudocode)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-9745-f9359ff438a3" class="">2.1 Identifiers and decidable equality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-adf5-dfd38b320bb3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">abbrev ClaimId := String
abbrev MetricId := String
abbrev EvidenceId := String
abbrev LicenseId := String
abbrev RewriteId := String</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-a942-df69c8824bad" class="">Lean requires <code>DecidableEq</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801a-bd07-fe6e3d9eed94" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">instance : DecidableEq ClaimId := inferInstance
-- similarly for others</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-9e44-e872a1c2de02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-92f7-f2dea95cb073" class="">2.2 Enumerations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-8b06-f439b24f9545" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive SupportType
| Emp | Inf | Def | MB | Prim | Lim
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

inductive Quant
| ALL | EXISTS | SOME | NONE | MOST | EXISTS_NOT
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

inductive Ownership
| Self
| Public
| Licensed (id : LicenseId)
| Unknown
| Restricted (id : LicenseId)
deriving DecidableEq, Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-bfa9-df866bf20d5e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8604-ce3e7475455e" class="">2.3 Failure codes and total order</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8319-d02b68632847" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive FailCode
| F0001 | F0002 | F0003
| F0101 | F0102 | F0103 | F0104 | F0105 | F0106
| F0201 | F0202 | F0203 | F0204
| F0301 | F0302 | F0303
| F0401 | F0402 | F0403
| F0501 | F0502
| F0601 | F0602
deriving DecidableEq, Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b6c9-d3b7709a391d" class="">Define a rank function for total order:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-bb37-da1c3c064bde" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def rank : FailCode → Nat
| .F0001 =&gt; 1
| .F0002 =&gt; 2
-- ...
| .F0602 =&gt; 999

def prec (a b : FailCode) : Prop := rank a &lt; rank b

theorem prec_total : ∀ a b, a = b ∨ prec a b ∨ prec b a := by
