  -- follows from Nat linear order on rank</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-b631-ee107aba4cc4" class="">This is the simplest way to enforce A4 in proof assistant form.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-a159-cf013a57e1d7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-b9a5-dfe7b9eb3f6d" class="">3) Normal form and contradiction (decidable by construction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-9b73-fde5ac4e82bf" class="">3.1 Literals and context compatibility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-8e31-c01c9e6c4251" class="">To avoid SAT, define a finite literal vocabulary with complement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-80d2-dc9f3dac3854" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive Lit
| L1 | L2 | L3 -- finite vocabulary
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def negLit : Lit → Lit
| .L1 =&gt; .L2
| .L2 =&gt; .L1
| .L3 =&gt; .L3  -- if self-negating is allowed; else remove</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-acd3-d8ceb3fa97e9" class="">Context is a finite set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b31b-f4d9a225a6f8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">abbrev Ctx := Finset Lit

def compatCtx (a b : Ctx) : Bool :=
  -- no ℓ in a such that negLit ℓ in b
  decide (¬ ∃ l, l ∈ a ∧ negLit l ∈ b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-8bd1-f6f480928e5c" class="">Because <code>Finset</code> is finite, this is decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9704-df78d4b89f75"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-aed9-d64157e2e141" class="">3.2 Intervals and overlap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-920f-ebdc2fb55af7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Interval where
  start : Int
  stop  : Int
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def ovT (i j : Interval) : Bool :=
  decide (i.start ≤ j.stop ∧ j.start ≤ i.stop)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-acc2-f06d95b3b263"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-879f-fb7f7cf4dec1" class="">3.3 Predicates, negation, mutex tables</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-bfbd-d4e66ba4472c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive PredSym
| P1 | P2 | P3
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def negPred : PredSym → PredSym
| .P1 =&gt; .P2
| .P2 =&gt; .P1
| .P3 =&gt; .P3</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b78f-de52a4923827" class="">Objects are also finite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-8910-e6b9e0b1b442" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive ObjSym
| O1 | O2 | O3
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def mutex : PredSym → ObjSym → ObjSym → Bool
| p, o1, o2 =&gt; decide (False) -- to be instantiated via table</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-8970-dcc994e55c7e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-aa4a-d19445c6216a" class="">3.4 Normal form and contradiction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-a1f9-fca442fe0adf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure NF where
  subj : Finset String
  pred : PredSym
  obj  : ObjSym
  quant : Quant
  time : Interval
  ctx  : Ctx
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def ovS (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  decide (∃ x, x ∈ a.subj ∧ x ∈ b.subj)

def compat (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  ovT a.time b.time &amp;&amp; compatCtx a.ctx b.ctx &amp;&amp; ovS a b</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-9e4f-f306f7d305b7" class="">Neg-pair:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-80f8-facae37a1bfc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def negPair (a b : NF) : Bool := decide (b.pred = negPred a.pred)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-8f64-c0f1201eb103" class="">Contradiction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-929e-c05755c58067" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def Con (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  let base := compat a b
  let negcase := negPair a b &amp;&amp; decide (a.obj = b.obj)
  let mutexcase := decide (a.pred = b.pred) &amp;&amp; mutex a.pred a.obj b.obj
  let quantcase :=
    decide (a.pred = b.pred ∧ a.obj = b.obj) &amp;&amp;
    decide ((a.quant = .ALL ∧ b.quant = .EXISTS_NOT) ∨ (b.quant = .ALL ∧ a.quant = .EXISTS_NOT))
  base &amp;&amp; (negcase || mutexcase || quantcase)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b788-d2022bd47c9d" class="">All of this is decidable and finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-92cb-fb366f9b7ca8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-9b78-c0665de4a4ea" class="">4) Claims, graphs, SCC</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-b451-e37b8d192e92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Claim where
  id : ClaimId
  nf : NF
  stype : SupportType
  deps : List ClaimId
  evidence : List EvidenceId
  metrics : List MetricId
  own : Ownership
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

structure Graph where
  V : List Claim
  A : List (ClaimId × ClaimId)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-ab01-e81aa5d79ff5" class="">Well-formedness:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9657-fc64aab7e843" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">edges reference known ids</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9bd9-f3465ccc512d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deps</code> aligns with <code>A</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-b3b4-d68f59e1285f" class="">SCC: use a verified algorithm or treat SCC detection as an axiomatically-correct function with a proof obligation later.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-b5ac-dd888ba80f7f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-9b7b-dbce3818e3dd" class="">5) Invariants (as decidable predicates returning codes)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-af32-c9612005f02e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure InvResult where
  pass : Bool
  code : Option FailCode
  deriving Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-8c65-e1c5025c30c6" class="">Example invariant I001:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-9a22-c6b2361cfe8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def I001_noContradiction (g : Graph) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ ci cj, ci ∈ g.V ∧ cj ∈ g.V ∧ ci.id ≠ cj.id ∧ Con ci.nf cj.nf)
  then ⟨false, some .F0001⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-9973-d565d0aecce0" class="">Ownership invariant I401:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9408-c5c6c9d3d98b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def I401_ownDeclared (g : Graph) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ c, c ∈ g.V ∧ c.own = .Unknown)
  then ⟨false, some .F0401⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b7d7-c84610ddf5b3" class="">Every invariant follows this pattern.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-b0c5-c28f32c8917d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-be4a-ce8eb21e7d14" class="">6) Failcode selection (minimum under rank)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-af66-d63eb6707474" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def collectCodes (rs : List InvResult) : List FailCode :=
  rs.foldr (fun r acc =&gt;
    match r.pass, r.code with
    | true, _ =&gt; acc
    | false, some c =&gt; c :: acc
    | false, none =&gt; acc
  ) []

def minCode (cs : List FailCode) : Option FailCode :=
  match cs with
  | [] =&gt; none
  | c :: rest =&gt;
    some (rest.foldl (fun m x =&gt; if rank x &lt; rank m then x else m) c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-b690-e40a6cd6534f" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-ab9b-c86832b99ab7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def failcode (rs : List InvResult) : Option FailCode :=
  minCode (collectCodes rs)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a9d8-cdcf8a0c8706" class="">Proof: <code>failcode = none ↔ all pass</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-b182-ddf3614fa1fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-8c89-f0f10a4d5829" class="">7) Rewrites with write-set proofs (core formal step)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-bd82-fa6d4e5ed980" class="">Define state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-ae83-c7788f433185" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure State where
  g : Graph
  -- P, Λ, M, Ω, L, b, etc.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-a1f7-ec2ee8b403e2" class="">Rewrite function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-9139-fe88d92730b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def R001_resolveContradiction (s : State) : State :=
  -- deterministic choice of the highest-ranked contradiction pair
  s -- placeholder</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-956e-e04477f549a7" class="">Write-set spec as a predicate over states:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b006-cc7d4d44f3cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def W001 (before after : State) : Prop :=
  -- only Claim.scope/type/limits/Λ allowed; expressed as “everything else equal”
  True -- placeholder</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-96eb-f8cf9e76b4c5" class="">Proof obligation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-bf1e-cb8bbe1b3638" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem R001_write_ok : ∀ s, W001 s (R001_resolveContradiction s) := by
  intro s; simp [W001, R001_resolveContradiction]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-888c-eb2f21902901" class="">You do this for each rewrite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-98ef-c58b0aae68c4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-9b45-fee2c308ae8c" class="">8) Defect measure and termination</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-adda-c5bc23153ecf" class="">Measure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-abfb-d4960e3e0b82" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def defects (s : State) : Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat :=
  -- counts of violation classes
  (0,0,0,0,0,0,0) -- placeholder</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-ab7e-e04051048cc8" class="">Lex order is well-founded on tuples of Nat.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-8539-cfd0deaa7d7b" class="">Key lemma per failcode class:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8ad0-c6884dbd5ac3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem rewrite_decreases :
  ∀ s c,
    failcode (evalInvariants s) = some c →
    defects (Rstar c s) &lt;lex defects s := by
  -- case split on c, use rewrite-specific decrease lemma
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b087-f804e2215a42" class="">Termination:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-94bd-faef0840e519" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Plain Text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem terminates :
  ∀ s, ∃ n, (iterate step n s).sealed := by
  -- well-founded recursion on defects
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-833d-c7f342016dfc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-b29f-cd32b2596e6f" class="">XXXV. What this “More” layer adds beyond the previous one</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-8f8a-d4f90404f6be" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>A concrete way to <strong>avoid NP-hard SAT</strong> by restricting to finite literal complements.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-b465-d8b03e8d2106" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>A concrete way to enforce a <strong>total failcode order</strong> via <code>rank</code>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9d52-cfd013ba318a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Concrete encodings for <strong>decidable contradiction</strong> and <strong>decidable invariants</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a3b2-e3d227467523" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>A concrete pattern for <strong>write-set proofs</strong> in a proof assistant.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-90c6-f577b1462621" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Concrete scaffolding for <strong>well-founded termination proofs</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b23f-f10d426f2bfb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-ac2e-cf21996244df" class="">XXXVI. The next (deeper) unavoidable fork</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-b12a-d15a12f5b78d" class="">To proceed further than this, one of the following must be fixed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b3d3-f2ef0eb74e7b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>The exact finite vocabularies: <code>PredSym</code>, <code>ObjSym</code>, <code>Lit</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-a088-c15d8eb74fb7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Whether intervals use <code>Int</code> or a custom bounded time type</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a6c0-f635ff2d079d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Whether budgets are <code>Nat</code>, <code>Rat</code>, or <code>Real</code> (Lean requires extra analysis libs for reals)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-9e09-e9b37c393cc3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Whether SCC and hash are axiomatized or formally verified</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-984b-da17ba6c6492" class="">If you choose those, the next output can be a complete, internally consistent Lean skeleton with all placeholders replaced by concrete tables and a full theorem dependency chain without “sorry” except for the hardest lemmas.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
